For every detected return, R2M-Bench compares the revisit pair with two controls from the same rollout: a gap-matched non-revisit pair that measures generic temporal stability and a short-range pair that estimates short-horizon consistency. These comparisons produce \emph{MemoryGain} (MG), the revisit advantage over the temporal baseline, and the \emph{Normalized Memory Ratio} (NMR), which normalizes this advantage by the short-to-baseline dynamic range. R2M-Bench combines 100 reference scenes with three leave-and-return trajectories to form 300 instances and evaluates appearance fidelity, scene and object identity, local geometry, and persistent state. Across seven action-conditioned video world models, Overall NMR correlates with human consistency judgments at Spearman's $ρ=0.547$ (95\% CI $[0.45,0.63]$). Its within-model correlation magnitude with generated motion is $0.072$, compared with $0.207$ for raw revisit similarity, indicating that relative calibration substantially reduces the slow-motion shortcut. DreamX-World-Memo achieves the highest Overall NMR among the evaluated video models. Together, these results support same-rollout relative calibration as a practical way to distinguish revisit-specific consistency from generic temporal stability.

</details>


### [183] [PAWBench: How Far Are We from Probabilistically Aligned World Modeling?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27345)
*Yuandong Pu,Le Zhuo,Sayak Paul,Gabriel Jorge Menezes,Avram Đorđević,Shiyang Li,Yifan Zhou,Bin Fu,Wenlong Zhang,Junjun He,Yu Qiao,Yihao Liu,Jingbo Xing,Xi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出概率对齐的概念，将视频生成模型作为世界模型进行评估，要求其不仅能生成合理轨迹，还需复现同一初始条件下多种可能行为的分布。作者引入PAWBench基准与PAWEval评估协议，在50个场景和11个系统中发现没有任何模型能同时匹配参考概率并覆盖有效行为范围，并进一步检验了语言提示、噪声采样和训练能否重塑模型的预测分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型越来越被视为世界模型，但物理过程往往有多种演化方式，因此世界模型应满足分布层面的"概率对齐"要求。然而现有评估仅关注单个视频的合理性，未检验重复生成是否能恢复正确的行为分布，导致缺乏系统衡量视频生成器作为随机采样器能力的方法。

Method: 形式化定义概率对齐为世界模型的分布性准则；构建PAWBench基准（覆盖50个场景）；提出PAWEval评估协议，将重复视频生成转化为可能的物理行为上的经验分布，并与参考概率进行比较。

Result: 在50个场景和11个当前视频生成系统上，没有任何模型能同时匹配参考概率并恢复有效行为的完整范围，表明现有系统在分布层面与世界建模存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 当前视频生成器在分布层面上尚未达到概率对齐的世界建模标准。作者验证了语言提示、初始噪声采样和模型训练等干预手段对预测分布的影响，并认为该工作可作为迈向概率对齐世界建模的基础。

Abstract: Recent video generation models are increasingly framed as world models. Many physical processes can unfold in more than one valid way. Therefore, a world model should reproduce not only a plausible trajectory, but also the distribution of possible behaviors under the same initial observation and action. We call this distribution-level requirement probabilistic alignment. However, existing evaluations largely assess individual-video plausibility and do not test whether repeated generations recover the correct distribution. This raises a central question: how far are current video generators from probabilistically aligned world modeling? To answer it, we formalize probabilistic alignment as a distributional criterion for world models and introduce PAWBench, a benchmark for evaluating video generators as stochastic samplers of world dynamics. We further introduce PAWEval, an outcome-level protocol that converts repeated video rollouts into empirical distributions over possible physical behaviors. Across 50 scenarios and eleven current systems, no model consistently matches the reference probabilities while recovering the range of valid behaviors. Having established this gap, we test whether language prompts, initial noise sampling, or model training can reshape the model's predictive distribution. We believe our work can serve as a foundation for future efforts to move towards probabilistically aligned world modeling.

</details>


### [184] [KnockGS:interaction-Grounded Calibrationof Physical Gaussian Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27365)
*Chenchen Ge,Hanwen Shen,Bowen Jing,Jiyuan Cai,Xiaofeng Wang,Hongsen Lei,Weitao Zhou,Dandan Zhang,Haibao Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出KnockGS框架，从受已知外力作用的3D高斯物体动态中估计其弹性和密度尺度，将力诱导响应作为校准信号，冻结估计参数并回写模拟器以测试未见交互，实现参数恢复和响应级保真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物理集成3D高斯表示虽然能对可变形物体进行模拟和渲染，但假设材料参数已知或手动指定，限制了其在必须从观测动态中推断参数时的应用。

Method: 将物理模拟从单纯的前向过程转化为逆向校准过程：从观测动态中提取时间响应特征，据此估计弹性和密度两个材料尺度，冻结估计值并回写同一模拟器。

Result: 在五个留出材料目标上，该方法比响应检索、全局回归或固定默认材料更准确地恢复尺度；冻结估计在方向和大小不同的交互下仍具有预测性。

Conclusion: 交互响应蕴含足够信息来校准物理接地3D高斯表示中的材料尺度，是实现渲染外观与模拟响应一致的交互式PhysicalGS系统的第一步。

Abstract: Physics-integrated 3D Gaussian representations now allow reconstructed deformable objects to be simulated and rendered under explicit material models. Existing pipelines, however, assume that material parameters are known or manually specified, limiting their applicability when these parameters must be inferred from observed object dynamics. We propose KnockGS, an interaction-response PhysicalGS framework that estimates the elasticity and density scales of a 3D Gaussian object from its dynamics under a known applied force. Rather than treating physical simulation only as a forward process, we turn the force-induced response into a calibration signal: temporal response features are xtracted from the observed dynamics, the two material scales are estimated from those features, and the estimate is then frozen and written back into the same simulator so that it can be tested on an interaction it was never fitted to.We evaluate the framework on both parameter recovery and response-level fidelity. The estimated scales are compared against hidden ground truth, and the re-simulated object is measured against the target using 3D particle trajectories, response-curve statistics, and rendered-frame quality. Across five held-out material targets, our method recovers the scales substantially more accurately than response retrieval, global regression, or a fixed default material, and the frozen estimate remains predictive under interactions that differ in direction and in magnitude. Interaction response therefore carries enough information to calibrate material scales in physically grounded 3D Gaussian representations.Our study is a first step toward interactive PhysicalGS systems that calibrate a Gaussian asset whose rendered appearance and simulated response are consistent.

</details>


### [185] [Successive Capacity Growth: Task-Complexity-Driven Width and Depth Expansion for Vision Transformer Encoders in JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27367)
*Frederik Berenz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Successive Capacity Growth (SCG)方法，让JEPA世界模型编码器从极小规模（1头、2层、283K参数）开始，通过任务无关的"测试-验证"机制，在函数保持展开（function-preserving expansion）的约束下按需增量扩宽（增加注意力头）或加深（增加Transformer块）。配合Sketched Isotropic Gaussian Regularizer (SIGReg)防止语义维度坍塌。在60维多目标动力学任务上预测损失改善20.3%，参数效率比固定大模型高56倍；在2D导航任务中比固定大模型提升23%。在三种环境下均匹配或超越固定小基线，零误报扩展，函数保持精确（ratio=1.0, absolute difference=0.0）。结论是JEPA编码器无需预分配最大容量，可按任务需求逐步增长，实现显著的计算与数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA世界模型使用固定大小的Vision Transformer编码器，存在两方面问题：对简单任务过度配置（冗余资源），对复杂任务配置不足（能力欠缺），且注意力头之间存在显著冗余。核心动机是探索编码器能否不预先分配最大容量，而是随任务复杂度按需增长，从而打破"固定架构"的设计范式。

Method: 提出两种核心机制：(1) SCG——从最小编码器（1头、2层、283K参数）出发，通过任务无关的test-and-verify机制，利用函数保持展开安全试错：宽度增长增加注意力头以增强低级语义容量，深度增长增加Transformer块以支持高阶语义抽象，若预测损失未改善则回滚；(2) SIGReg——保证所有学习的语义维度在统计上独立且与预测目标对齐，防止在架构增长过程中出现表示坍塌。

Result: 在60维多目标动力学任务上，SCG自然触发深度扩展，比固定小基线提升20.3%预测损失，参数效率比扩展到固定大模型高56倍。在2D导航任务上，单次宽度扩展即比固定大模型提升23%。在三种递增复杂度的环境中，自适应编码器均匹配或超越固定小基线，零误报扩展（zero false-positive expansions），位精确函数保持（ratio=1.0，绝对差=0.0）。

Conclusion: JEPA世界模型编码器不应被预先分配为最大容量，而应随任务需求逐步增长。这种自适应增长策略在保持表示质量的同时，显著提升了计算与数据效率，为世界模型架构设计提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) for world modeling typically employ fixed-size Vision Transformer encoders that are over-provisioned for simple tasks and under-provisioned for complex ones, with significant redundancy across attention heads. We propose Successive Capacity Growth (SCG), a method that starts from a minimal encoder (1 head, 2 layers, 283K parameters) and grows incrementally in width (adding attention heads for low-level semantic capacity) or depth (adding transformer blocks for higher-order semantic abstraction), driven by a task-agnostic test-and-verify mechanism that exploits function-preserving expansion to safely trial architectural changes and roll back if they do not improve prediction loss. The Sketched Isotropic Gaussian Regularizer (SIGReg) ensures that all learned semantic dimensions remain statistically independent and aligned with the predictive objective, preventing collapse even as the architecture grows. On a 60-dimensional multi-object dynamics task, SCG naturally triggers depth expansion, improving prediction loss by 20.3% over the fixed small baseline with 56 times greater parameter efficiency than scaling to the fixed large model; on a 2D navigation task, a single width expansion yields even an 23% improvement over the fixed large model. Across all three tested environments of increasing complexity, the adaptive encoder matches or exceeds the fixed small baseline, with zero false-positive expansions and bit-exact function preservation (ratio = 1.0, absolute difference = 0.0). The take-away is that JEPA world model encoders need not be pre-allocated at maximum capacity - they can grow successively as the task demands, achieving significant compute and data efficiency while maintaining representation quality.

</details>


### [186] [LeVJEPA: Efficient & Scalable Video Pretraining without the Heuristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27395)
*Lukas Kuhn,Lucas Maes,Giuseppe Serra,Quentin Le Lidec,Yann LeCun,Randall Balestriero,Florian Buettner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LeVJEPA，是首个在LeJEPA无塌缩目标下训练的视频编码器。它去除了不对称网络结构（EMA目标编码器、stop-gradient、容量受限的预测器）和像素空间重建，仅用一个编码器加投影器，通过不变性损失配合SIGReg正则化即可防止表示塌缩。该方法通过随机token丢弃大幅降低预训练计算成本，在相同epoch下以5.6-20.8倍更少的计算量匹配或超越V-JEPA 2，且支持块因果注意力而不损失精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频包含物理世界的时间结构，但从视频中学习表示的计算成本一直很高。现有的自监督方法要么依赖不对称架构（EMA目标编码器、stop-gradient、容量受限预测器）来防止表示塌缩，要么通过像素空间重建掩码内容来规避塌缩，这两种方式都带来了高昂的计算开销。作者希望消除这些不必要的计算负担，使视频成为一种可行甚至更优的通用视觉预训练载体。

Method: LeVJEPA采用单一编码器架构，结合一个投影器，通过全局和局部视图之间的不变性损失进行训练，并使用SIGReg正则化来保证防止表示塌缩（具有可证明的保证）。目标函数仅包含单个超参数。训练成本由编码器观察的token数量决定，通过均匀随机token丢弃来减少计算量。此外，由于分支间不需要不对称性，编码器可采用块因果注意力（block-causal attention）训练，使时间顺序成为编码器自身的属性。

Result: 在相同epoch和相同数据上，LeVJEPA以5.6至20.8倍更少的预训练计算量匹配或超越V-JEPA 2（涵盖ViT-S/B/L架构）。在相同总FLOPs下，它在ImageNet-1K上超过最强视频基线7.6个点，同时在运动中心基准上保持竞争力。与计算匹配的DINOv2（在相同视频帧上训练）相比，LeVJEPA在表观中心评估上接近图像预训练编码器，而在运动中心精度上几乎翻倍。

Conclusion: 一旦消除了视频预训练的计算开销，视频就成为通用视觉预训练的一个可行且在多方面更优的载体。LeVJEPA证明了通过消除架构不对称和像素重建，可以将视频预训练的计算成本大幅降低，同时保持甚至提升下游任务的性能。

Abstract: Video carries the temporal structure of the physical world, yet learning representations from it has remained computationally expensive: prevailing self-supervised methods either prevent representation collapse through architectural asymmetries, coupling an exponential-moving-average target encoder, a stop-gradient, and a capacity-limited predictor, or circumvent it by reconstructing masked content in pixel space. We introduce LeVJEPA, the first video encoder trained under LeJEPA's collapse-free objective, which dispenses with both. A single encoder is trained with an invariance loss over global and local views of a clip, regularized by SIGReg, which excludes collapse with a provable guarantee. The architecture reduces to an encoder and a projector, and the objective to a single hyperparameter. This formulation admits two properties. First, the cost of pretraining is governed by the number of tokens the encoder observes; uniform random token dropping renders this number small while simultaneously improving downstream accuracy. At matched epochs on identical data, LeVJEPA matches or surpasses V-JEPA 2 across ViT-S/B/L at 5.6 to 20.8x less pretraining compute, and at matched total FLOPs it exceeds the strongest video baseline by 7.6 points on ImageNet-1K while remaining competitive on motion-centric benchmarks. Second, since no asymmetry between branches is required, the encoder can be trained with block-causal attention at no measurable accuracy cost: temporal ordering becomes a property of the encoder itself. Against a compute-matched DINOv2 trained on frames of the same videos, LeVJEPA approaches the image-pretrained encoder on appearance-centric evaluation while nearly doubling its motion-centric accuracy. These results indicate that, once its computational overhead is removed, video becomes a viable and in several respects preferable substrate for general-purpose visual pretraining.

</details>


### [187] [Reconstructing Humans and Objects in Interaction using Large Reconstruction Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27407)
*Agniv Chatterjee,Georgios Pavlakos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MILO是一个利用大型重建模型(LRMs)的视觉能力从单张图像恢复详细3D人-物交互框架。其核心思想是通过LRM获取保持人物相对布局和邻近线索的几何骨架，将3D HOI重建任务重新定义为对LRM网格的解释：分割人物和物体组件，对人物部分拟合参数化人体模型，并可选地将物体模板对齐到物体部分。实验表明MILO在多个基准和交互场景中实现强重建精度并优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D人-物交互重建是AR/VR、机器人和具身AI等应用的基础问题，但现有方法主要依赖重投影和接触约束，将参数化人体模型和物体模板拟合到2D图像，面临深度模糊、遮挡和物体形状多变性等挑战。作者希望探索一种新途径来克服这些限制。

Method: 作者提出MILO框架，利用大型重建模型(LRMs)的几何骨架能力：首先通过LRM从单张图像生成保留人物相对布局和邻近线索的3D网格，然后将问题重构为解释LRM网格——分割网格为人体和物体两部分，对人物部分拟合参数化人体模型，若存在可用物体模板则将其与物体部分对齐。

Result: MILO在多个基准数据集和不同交互场景中实现了强重建精度，并优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 利用LRM的几何先验可以显著简化单图像3D HOI重建流程，将问题从复杂的2D拟合转为3D网格解释，为3D人-物交互重建提供了新的有效思路。

Abstract: Estimation of Human-Object Interactions in 3D (3D HOI) is a fundamental problem in 3D computer vision with applications in AR/VR, robotics, and embodied AI. However, reconstructing these interactions in 3D remains challenging due to depth ambiguities, occlusions, and object shape variability. Existing approaches are primarily concerned with reprojection and contact constraints, fitting parametric human models and object templates to 2D images. In this paper, we explore a different avenue. We present MILO, a framework that leverages the visual capabilities of Large Reconstruction Models (LRMs) to recover detailed 3D human-object interactions from a single image. Our key observation is that LRMs provide a powerful geometric scaffold that preserves relative human-object arrangement and proximity cues. This significantly simplifies the reconstruction procedure, reframing the problem as interpreting the LRM mesh: we segment it into human and object components, fit a parametric body model to the human part, and optionally align an object template to the object part (if such a template is available). MILO achieves strong reconstruction accuracy and outperforms existing baselines across multiple benchmarks and interaction scenarios. Our code is available at https://ac5113.github.io/MILO.

</details>


### [188] [Retrieval Heads Meet Vision: Uncovering How VLMs Locate and Extract Visual Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27417)
*Chanho Park,Daehyeon Choi,Jihyun Lee,Minhyuk Sung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉检索头（VRHs），即视觉语言模型（VLMs）中约1.7-2.6%的一小部分注意力头，它们在因果上负责将文本描述锚定到图像区域。通过统一设计空间中的头部评分方法，研究发现仅屏蔽前20个VRHs即可将定位准确率降低高达80个百分点。VRHs不仅复现了文本检索头的因果-稀疏-通用三元组，还展现出跨任务泛化、功能特异性及跨模型架构共享等新特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型能够定位文本提示所指的图像区域并将相应视觉证据路由到输出，但其内部机制尚不明确。受大语言模型中检索头的启发，研究者质疑VLMs是否包含类似的视觉检索机制，并希望揭示其因果机制。

Method: 将现有头部评分方法重新设计到统一的设计空间中，涵盖查询词元、键聚合和跨样本聚合三个维度。通过从输出预测词元计算注意力得分并对真值引用区域求和来识别因果头部。在11个VLMs和5个指代表达基准上验证。

Result: 仅屏蔽前20个VRHs即可将定位准确率降低高达80个百分点，而屏蔽相同数量的随机头部影响甚微。VRHs展现出三种新属性：跨视觉参考任务泛化（包括属性、空间、计数和视觉数学任务）、功能特异性（破坏定位但保留输出格式）、架构共享性（在共享LLM主干但视觉编码器、投影器和指令微调不同的VLM之间可因果迁移）。

Conclusion: VLMs中存在与文本检索头类似的视觉检索机制，并且VRHs展示了超越此前报告的因果-稀疏-通用三元组的更丰富性质，这对理解和解释VLM的视觉-语言对齐机制具有重要启示。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can locate an image region referred to by a text prompt and route the corresponding visual evidence to the output, yet the internal mechanism behind this behavior is not understood. Inspired by retrieval heads in large language models, we ask whether VLMs contain an analogous mechanism for visual retrieval. We answer affirmatively by introducing Visual Retrieval Heads (VRHs), a small subset of attention heads (about 1.7-2.6%) that are causally responsible for grounding text descriptions to image regions. To find them, we recast existing head-scoring methods under a unified design space over query tokens, key aggregation, and cross-sample aggregation. We then show that scoring attention from output prediction tokens with a sum over the ground-truth referent region most reliably identifies causal heads. Across eleven VLMs and five referring-expression benchmarks, masking only the top 20 VRHs reduces grounding accuracy by up to 80 percentage points, while masking the same number of random heads has little effect. Beyond replicating the causal-sparse-universal triad established for text retrieval heads, VRHs exhibit several properties not previously reported: they generalize across visual reference tasks, remaining causal on attribute, spatial, counting, and visual-math benchmarks despite being discovered through bounding-box prediction; they are functionally specific, preserving output format while corrupting localization; and they are architecturally shared, transferring causally across VLMs that share an LLM backbone but differ in vision encoder, projector, and instruction tuning.

</details>


### [189] [UrbanGround: From Local Perception to Spatial Agency in a Real-Scale City](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27456)
*Tianjie Ju,Zheng Wu,Yueqing Sun,Yuhan Cui,Bobo Li,Shengqiong Wu,Pengzhou Cheng,Haodong Zhao,Zongru Wu,Xinbei Ma,Doris Zhang,Kunling Li,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu,Hao Fei,Qi Gu,Gongshen Liu,Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UrbanGround，一个基于香港真实3D地理空间数据构建的物理约束城市沙盒，用于系统测试多模态大语言模型(MLLM)智能体能否将局部城市感知转化为可靠行动。研究发现当代MLLM智能体在原子级视觉识别和短距离空间推理上表现出色，但方向感、行人感知和长距离导航中的误差累积与自我纠错能力存在严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM智能体可以理解街景图像，但城市智能体（urban agency）的核心在于移动过程中本地证据是否持续有效。当前缺乏一个能在大规模真实城市尺度下测试MLLM智能体闭环交互能力的基准环境，因此无法系统回答"智能体能否将局部感知转化为可靠行动"这一关键问题。

Method: 提出UrbanGround沙盒平台，基于香港全境3D地理空间数据构建物理约束的城市复制环境。平台支持第一人称视角的闭环交互和交互式导航地图，智能体可直接进入3D城市从第一视角探索。研究设计三个递进的研究问题：(1)主动观察后能否回答空间问题；(2)目的地变远且不明确时导航能力如何；(3)路线可用性和行人运动变化时行为是否稳健。

Result: 当代MLLM智能体在视觉识别和短距离空间推理等原子能力上表现可用的水平，但方向感知和行人感知运动仍然不可靠。核心失败模式在于扩展探索过程中，局部能力无法组合为持续的目标导向行为，误差不断累积且缺乏有效的纠错机制。

Conclusion: UrbanGround为研究MLLM智能体在复杂开放城市环境中可靠探索能力的边界提供了测试平台。当前智能体在长距离、开放式的城市导航任务中尚不可靠，希望该沙盒能支持更广泛的研究，推动MLLM智能体在城市环境中的可靠部署。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can interpret a street view, but urban agency depends on whether such local evidence remains useful after the agent starts to move. In this paper, we investigate how far current MLLM agents can turn local urban perception into reliable action in a complicated real-scale city. We propose UrbanGround, the first sandbox to make this question testable in a physically constrained replica of Hong Kong built from territory-wide 3D geospatial data. UrbanGround supports closed-loop interaction from a first-person view and provides an interactive map for navigation. Agents can directly enter the 3D city and explore from a first-person view. Our analysis follows the growth of the spatial problem through three research questions. We first test whether an agent can ground a local scene well enough to answer spatial questions after active observation. Then we ask whether that grounding supports navigation as destinations become farther away and less explicit. Finally, we examine whether the resulting behavior survives changes in route availability and pedestrian motion. Contemporary MLLM agents usually show useful atomic abilities in visual recognition and short-range spatial reasoning, while orientation and pedestrian-aware movement remain unreliable. Their central failure emerges over extended exploration, where local abilities do not compose into sustained goal-directed behavior and errors accumulate without effective correction. We hope UrbanGround will support broader study of how far current MLLM agents can explore reliably in complex, open-ended urban environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [ADeptS-Bench: Measuring the Trustworthiness of Computer Use Agents Across Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26204)
*Joy Chen,Alejandro Castillejo Munoz,Pierluca D'Oro,Yuxuan Sun,Chloe Evans,Joseph Tighe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ADepts-Bench，一个双流可信度基准，用于评估计算机使用代理（CUA）在视觉界面中的安全交互能力（安全流）和歧义指令下的澄清能力（消歧流）。评估七个模型发现，没有模型能同时达到80%任务成功率且攻击成功率低于30%，所有模型都会毫不犹豫点击2.5万美元订单的"结算"按钮，且无人发现"恢复出厂设置"按钮被错误标注为"优化"。消融实验揭示了三种不同的安全架构类型，且所有模型在消歧任务中普遍高估后果严重性，反映出过度拒绝偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用代理正被广泛部署用于替用户操作移动和桌面应用，但现有基准缺乏对两个关键维度的全面评估：一是能否在视觉界面中安全交互（抵御嵌入在界面中的威胁），二是面对歧义指令时是否会主动寻求澄清。现有基准要么只关注任务完成能力，要么只关注文本层面的安全，没有覆盖视觉界面中的威胁与歧义处理。

Method: 基于ADEPTS能力框架和普通人群用户研究，构建双流可信度基准。安全流提供配对的良性/恶意任务，将威胁嵌入视觉界面中（如图标误标、恶意按钮）；消歧流评估代理在意图不明确时是否主动寻求澄清。对七个模型进行全面评估，并通过消融实验分析不同安全架构的机制差异。

Result: 评估结果显示：没有模型能一致性地在任务成功率超过80%的同时保持攻击成功率低于30%；所有模型都会在2.5万美元订单上无犹豫地点击"结算"按钮；没有模型能检测出"恢复出厂设置"按钮被误标为"优化"。消融实验揭示了三种安全架构：工具依赖型（移除拒绝工具后攻击成功率提升21-23个百分点）、部分工具依赖型（提升10-11个百分点）和无安全机制型（攻击成功率不变）。消歧方面，所有模型都高估后果严重性，与安全领域的过度拒绝偏见一致。

Conclusion: 当前CUA模型在两个关键可信度维度上均存在显著不足：视觉界面威胁检测能力薄弱，且普遍存在过度拒绝/高估后果的偏见。研究为CUA安全评估提供了新的基准框架，并为安全架构设计提供了分类学视角，所有数据和代码将公开发布以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Computer Use Agents (CUAs) are increasingly deployed to navigate mobile and desktop applications on behalf of users, yet no benchmark comprehensively evaluates whether they can safely interact with visual interfaces while handling ambiguous instructions. We introduce ADeptS-Bench, a dual-stream trustworthiness benchmark, grounded in the ADEPTS capability framework and general population user studies. The Safety stream provides paired benign/malicious tasks with threats embedded in the visual interface. The Disambiguation stream evaluates whether agents seek clarification when intent is ambiguous. Evaluating seven models reveals that no model consistently exceeds 80% task success while staying below 30% attack success; every model clicks "Checkout" on a $25K order without hesitation, and none detects that a "factory reset" button is mislabeled as "Optimize." An ablation reveals three distinct safety architectures: tool-dependent (ASR +21-23pp without refusal tool), partially tool-dependent (+10-11pp), and no mechanism (unchanged). In disambiguation, all models overestimate consequence severity, mirroring the over-refusal bias observed in safety. We release all data, evaluation code, and analysis tools upon publication.

</details>


### [191] [How Do LLM Agents Actually Get the Flag? Trace-Level Provenance for Agentic Offensive Security Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26237)
*Kimberly Milner,Minghao Shao,Nanda Rani,Haoran Xi,Venkata Sai Charan Putrevu,Meet Udeshi,Sandeep K. Shukla,Prashanth Krishnamurthy,Farshad Khorrami,Muhammad Shafique,Ramesh Karri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CTF-ABACUS 是一个基于轨迹的代理审计框架，通过将代理行为分解为渗透测试阶段和分类技术，重建基于证据的解题档案，以区分真实漏洞利用与捷径（如flag直接暴露、记忆召回、外部查找、猜测等）。对1,435次CTF尝试的分析显示，轨迹验证的真实利用仅占恢复flag的62-87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CTF基准在评估自主语言模型代理的攻防安全能力时仅依赖浅层二元判断或聚合分数，忽略了代理到达flag的轨迹。这导致真实漏洞利用与flag直接暴露、记忆召回、外部查找、猜测及无依据声称相混淆，可能高估代理的网络安全能力。

Method: 引入CTF-ABACUS框架：将每次运行重建为基于证据的解题档案，把代理动作分解为渗透测试阶段和分类技术，识别漏洞利用发生位置、flag首次出现位置，以及恢复的flag是否被演示行为所支持。跨代理聚合解题档案生成挑战签名，以区分成功源于预期漏洞利用还是捷径路径。

Result: 对六个前沿和开源模型在240个挑战上的1,435次CTF尝试应用CTF-ABACUS，在两种评判视角下生成2,870份解题档案。轨迹验证的漏洞利用仅占跨基准恢复flag的62-87%，而捷径恢复遵循明显更浅的轨迹。

Conclusion: 研究将CTF评估从"计数恢复的flag"转向"验证演示的漏洞利用"，为设计能更好隔离攻防能力的基准提供了基础。

Abstract: Capture-the-Flag (CTF) benchmarks are widely used to assess the offensive security capabilities of autonomous language-model agents. Evaluations rely on shallow binary judgments or aggregate scores, overlooking the agent's trajectory to the flag. Consequently actual exploitation is conflated with direct flag exposure, memorized recall, external lookup, guessing, and unsupported claims, potentially overstating the agent's cybersecurity capability. We introduce CTF-ABACUS, a trace-based agent auditing framework that reconstructs each run as an evidence-grounded solve profile. By decomposing agent actions into penetration-testing phases and categorical techniques, it identifies where exploitation occurs, where the flag first appears, and whether the recovered flag is supported by demonstrated behavior. Aggregating solve profiles across agents yields challenge signatures that reveal whether success was achieved via the intended exploit or via shortcut pathways. We apply CTF-ABACUS to 1,435 CTF attempts by six frontier and open-source models on 240 challenges, yielding 2,870 solve profiles under two judge lenses. Trace-verified exploits account for only 62-87% of recovered flags across benchmarks, while shortcut recoveries follow substantially shallower trajectories. These findings shift CTF evaluation from counting recovered flags to verifying demonstrated exploitation and provide a basis for designing benchmarks that better isolate the offensive capabilities.

</details>


### [192] [FRESCO: Complete and Scalable Temporal Safety for CHERI Application Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26353)
*Merve Gülmez,Nils Jordan,Jialun Zhang,Hossam ElAtali,Gang Tan,N. Asokan,Thomas Nyman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FRESCO是一个CHERI硬件/软件协同设计系统，通过为栈指针添加"调用实例来源标识符"（颜色）来提供硬件强制执行的栈使用后返回（use-after-return）防护，同时通过Color Saver静态分析和能力颜色分段技术解决颜色压力问题，实现完整的时空内存安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CHERI提供了硬件强制的空间内存安全，前序工作扩展了堆的时间安全，但栈上的use-after-return问题仍未被解决。现有防御手段存在明显不足：编译器分析只能可靠捕获作为函数返回值逃逸的引用，而动态净化器（sanitizer）的开销过高，无法用于生产环境部署。

Method: FRESCO遵循"栈能力不得比创建它的栈帧存活更久"的原则。具体方法包括：1) 为栈指针添加每次调用的来源标识符（"着色"），从栈指针派生的每个能力都继承该生命周期，函数退出时硬件立即使其失效，无论逃逸方式与位置；2) Color Saver——一种静态的、能力感知的逃逸分析，限制仅对需要的函数着色，其核心算法在Rocq中进行了机械验证；3) 能力颜色分段——将内存划分为不相交的分段，每段拥有独立的颜色命名空间，使栈与堆的时间安全能够共存。

Result: FRESCO在CHERI-RISC-V QEMU全系统模拟器和乱序CHERI-Toooba FPGA软核上实现，软件支持来自CHERI Clang/LLVM编译器和CheriBSD OS。该系统在NIST Juliet测试套件和CVE上系统性防止了use-after-return、use-after-free和double-free，SPEC CPU运行开销仅4%（g.m.），SQLite和PostgreSQL为10-14%。

Conclusion: FRESCO通过硬件/软件协同设计，首次为CHERI应用处理器提供了完整且可扩展的时间安全（同时覆盖栈和堆），实现了对use-after-return、use-after-free和double-free的系统性防护，且运行时开销保持在可接受的低水平（4-14%），具备生产部署的潜力。

Abstract: CHERI provides hardware-enforced spatial memory safety. While prior work extends it with heap temporal safety, stack use-after-return remains unaddressed. Existing defenses fall short: compiler analysis reliably catches only references that escape as function return values, while dynamic sanitizers impose overheads that preclude production deployment.
  We present FRESCO, built on the principle that a stack capability must not outlive the frame that created it. FRESCO "colors" the stack pointer with per-invocation provenance identifiers; every capability derived from it inherits that lifetime and is hardware-invalidated the moment the function exits, regardless of how or where it escaped.
  Because stack frames retire orders of magnitude more frequently than heap allocations, FRESCO manages the resulting color pressure through: 1) Color Saver, a static capability-aware escape analysis that confines coloring to functions needing it, and whose core algorithm we mechanically verify in Rocq, and 2) capability-color segmentation, which partitions memory into disjoint segments, each with an independent color namespace. Color segmentation lets stack and heap temporal safety coexist on one system, making FRESCO the first hardware/software co-design to provide complete and scalable temporal safety for CHERI application processors.
  We realize FRESCO on the CHERI-RISC-V QEMU full-system emulator and the out-of-order CHERI-Toooba FPGA softcore, with software support in the CHERI-enabled Clang/LLVM compiler and CheriBSD OS. FRESCO systematically prevents use-after-return, use-after-free, and double-free across the NIST Juliet Test Suite and CVEs, with only a small run-time overhead in SPEC CPU (4% g.m.), SQLite, and PostgreSQL (10-14%).

</details>


### [193] [PRISM: Lightweight Enclave Isolation with Prismatic Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26367)
*Merve Gülmez,Adam Caulfield,Hakan Englund,N. Asokan,Thomas Nyman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PRISM是一种基于CHERI的可信执行环境（TEE）架构，通过引入"prismatic capabilities"机制，在用户态创建enclave，解决了CHERI TEE方案中存在的四个关键问题：昂贵的capability撤销操作、对宿主OS的依赖（provenance追踪和物理内存保护）、昂贵的域切换开销、以及缺乏远程认证支持。PRISM将enclave的"hue"标识符绑定到物理内存访问控制上，实现O(1)的所有权建立（无需内存扫描），支持高效的域切换（原子性地激活/停用prismatic capabilities），并支持远程认证。实验表明用户态enclave执行开销仅为<=15%，显著优于相同工作负载在Intel SGX下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TEE能保护敏感代码和数据免受外部干扰，但缺乏固有的内存安全；CHERI能提供对象级别的空间内存安全。然而，现有基于CHERI原语构建TEE的方案存在四大缺陷：(1) capability撤销操作开销昂贵，(2) 需要依赖宿主OS支持provenance追踪和物理内存保护，(3) 域切换开销大，(4) 不支持远程认证。作者旨在设计一种新的TEE架构来同时解决这些问题。

Method: 作者引入"prismatic capabilities"概念，并基于此设计PRISM架构。PRISM将enclave的"hue"（标识符）绑定到物理内存访问控制上，使得可以无需内存扫描即可实现O(1)的所有权建立；通过原子性地激活和停用prismatic capabilities来实现高效的域切换；并设计远程认证机制以支持enclave的可验证性。

Result: PRISM执行用户态enclave的开销仅为适中水平（<=15%），相比相同工作负载在Intel SGX下的性能，PRISM表现出显著的优势。

Conclusion: PRISM通过prismatic capabilities成功地将CHERI的内存安全机制与TEE的隔离需求相结合，在解决现有CHERI TEE方案四大痛点的同时，保持了较低的运行时开销，并具备远程认证能力，是比Intel SGX更高效的TEE方案。

Abstract: Trusted execution environments (TEEs) protect sensitive code and data from external interference, but lack inherent memory safety. CHERI can enforce spatial memory safety at the object level. Attempts to establish a TEE using CHERI primitives suffer from (1) expensive capability revocation operations, (2) need to rely on the host operating system (OS) to support provenance tracking and physical memory protection, (3) expensive domain transitions, and (4) lack of support for remote attestation.
  We introduce prismatic capabilities and present PRISM, a TEE architecture for CHERI leveraging prismatic capabilities to create userspace enclaves while addressing these challenges. PRISM binds enclave 'hues' (identifiers) in prismatic capabilities to physical memory access controls, enables O(1) ownership establishment without memory sweeps, and supports efficient domain transitions that atomically activate and deactivate prismatic capabilities. Additionally, PRISM enables remote attestation of its enclaves. We demonstrate that execution of userspace enclaves in PRISM incurs only moderate overhead (<= 15%), a significant improvement over the same workloads under Intel SGX

</details>


### [194] [Improving the Robustness of the XRP Ledger Network via Edge Augmentation Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26380)
*Afonso Vilalonga,Orkun İrsoy,João S. Resende,Henrique Domingos,Osman Yağan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了XRP Ledger区块链网络共识协议的鲁棒性提升策略。通过对比增边（augmentation）和重连（rewiring）两种拓扑优化策略，发现增边策略在少量增加边（每节点3条）的情况下即可达到甚至超过重连策略的鲁棒性指标，且K-out随机增边策略在保持原网络拓扑相似性方面表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XRP Ledger的共识协议要求参与者的唯一节点列表（UNL）之间有显著重叠，且每个UNL内需要高达80%的共识阈值。这使得网络容易受到针对关键节点的攻击——攻击者可以降低可信节点间的通信量，使其低于所需阈值，从而破坏共识过程。因此，需要评估和改进现有网络拓扑的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出正式的quorum鲁棒性和网络鲁棒性定义，并比较两类拓扑优化策略：(1) 增边策略——基于不同构造方式添加新边（包括随机K-out策略）；(2) 重连/边替换策略——保持总边数不变但重新排列边。每种策略考虑两种场景：所有节点参与构造/重连 vs.仅部分节点参与。

Result: 增边策略在鲁棒性提升上显著优于默认XRP Ledger拓扑；部分增边策略即使每条节点仅增加3条边，也能达到或超过重连策略的鲁棒性指标。此外，随机K-out增边策略相比重连策略，在Jaccard相似度指标上能更好地保持与原网络的拓扑相似性。

Conclusion: 增边策略是提升XRP Ledger网络鲁棒性的更优选择，尤其是在小规模边增加的情况下就能获得显著效果，且相比重连策略更能保持原有网络结构特征。

Abstract: The XRP Ledger allows its network participants to select a set of trusted peers within the network (i.e., the Unique Node List (UNL)) and communicate with them to reach consensus on which transactions should be included in the next ledger state. However, its consensus protocol requires significant overlap among participants' UNLs, along with a high agreement threshold among the nodes within each UNL (e.g., 80\%). Consequently, an attacker could disrupt the consensus process in such a network by targeting the nodes that form the network's connectivity backbone and reducing the number of trusted participants that can communicate with one another below the required threshold. In this paper, we evaluate strategies to improve the robustness of the XRP Ledger's existing topology, as measured by our formal definitions of quorum and network robustness, and compare them to a second strategy from prior work. The strategy we present is an addition/augmentation approach, in which new edges are added based on different constructions. The second strategy is a rewiring or edge-replacement approach, in which the overall number of edges is preserved but they are rearranged. For each strategy, we consider two different cases: one in which all nodes participate in the edge construction or rewiring process, and another in which only a subset of nodes participates. Our findings demonstrate substantial improvements in robustness when augmentation strategies are used over the default XRP Ledger topology and show that some augmentation strategies achieve robustness metrics equal to or exceeding the rewiring strategy, even when the number of edges added is small (e.g., three edges per node). Additionally, we show that the random K-out-based augmentation strategy maintains higher topological similarity to the original network than rewiring, as measured by Jaccard similarity.

</details>


### [195] [SILK: Closing the Time-of-Check-to-Time-of-Use Gap in RoT-Protected AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26402)
*Ruichen Qi,Xinting Jiang,Ema Dimitrova,Junyi Luo,Quan Cheng,Mehdi Saligane*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SILK是一种在最终计算边界前验证权重流的轻量级完整性机制，通过重用量化权重LSB作为密钥完整性位并链式依赖权重字节，无需额外认证标签即可检测流篡改，防止TOCTOU完整性漏洞。在Xilinx ZCU102上以极低的面积成本实现高吞吐验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RoT（信任根）认证仅在DNN模型加载时验证权重，但权重在加载后可能经DRAM、DMA、互连和预取路径到达计算引擎的途中被篡改，形成TOCTOU（检查时间到使用时间）完整性缺口，导致认证后的模型图像未变但实际消费的权重已被改变。

Method: SILK通过以下方式实现完整性保护：1）将量化权重的LSB重新用作密钥化完整性位；2）跨权重字节链式依赖，使局部修改扰动多个完整性检查；3）轻量级流式检查器在无需独立认证标签的情况下重算这些检查；4）采用提交门控（commit gating）阻止未验证权重进入计算。

Result: 在安全PRF下，伪造概率随受影响检查数量呈指数下降，实测命中率与解析界紧密吻合。SILK检测到功能攻击套件中所有流篡改实例。INT8在CNN上质量损失最多0.76个百分点、八个LLM上困惑度损失最多0.17；INT4和MXFP4通过检查稀疏性提供可配置的安全-质量权衡。在Xilinx ZCU102上，流水线实现以1.00%的Caliptra 2.x RoT等效面积成本维持756 MB/s，保守每次尝试伪造界2^-128的配置在6.15%面积成本下维持678 MB/s。

Conclusion: SILK通过将完整性检查放置在最终预计算边界处并利用量化权重LSB进行内联验证，有效弥合了ROT认证后的TOCTOU完整性缺口，在极低的硬件面积开销下提供可证明的安全性保障和可配置的安全-质量权衡。

Abstract: Root-of-trust (RoT) authentication verifies a DNN model at load time, but weights may subsequently traverse DRAM, DMA, interconnect, and prefetch paths before reaching the compute engine. Post-verification tampering along this path can therefore alter the weights actually consumed while leaving the authenticated model image unchanged, creating a time-of-check-to-time-of-use (TOCTOU) integrity gap.
  We present SILK (Streaming Inline Lightweight Keying), an in-place integrity mechanism that verifies the weight stream at the final pre-compute boundary. SILK repurposes quantized-weight LSBs as secret-keyed integrity bits and chains dependencies across weight bytes, so a local modification perturbs multiple integrity checks. A lightweight streaming checker recomputes these checks without separate authentication tags and uses commit gating to prevent unverified weights from reaching computation. Under a secure pseudorandom function (PRF), the forgery probability decreases exponentially with the number of affected checks, and measured miss rates closely track the analytical bound. SILK detects every stream-modifying instance in our functional attack suite. For INT8, it limits quality loss to at most 0.76 pp across evaluated CNNs and 0.17 perplexity across eight LLMs, while INT4 and MXFP4 provide a configurable security-quality tradeoff through check sparsity. On a Xilinx ZCU102, the synthesized reference pipelined implementation sustains 756 MB/s at only 1.00% of the equivalent area cost of a Caliptra 2.x RoT, while a configuration with a conservative per-attempt forgery bound of 2^-128 still sustains 678 MB/s at 6.15% of the RoT cost.

</details>


### [196] [IoMT-SecAlarmBench: A Counterfactual Benchmark for Integrity Attacks in IoMT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26416)
*Emmanuel C. Ugwuabonyi,Dmitri Perkins*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出IoMT-SecAlarmBench，一个半合成基准数据集，通过向真实ECG+PPG信号中注入受控完整性攻击来评估物联网医疗设备入侵检测方法。研究发现现有检测器在重放攻击和低幅瞬态尖峰等难例上接近随机水平，且检测攻击与区分传感器故障之间存在性能权衡，同时揭示现有IoMT入侵检测性能部分源于身份识别信息的泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoMT中异常读数可能来自真实生理事件、设备故障或网络攻击，但现有数据集缺乏反事实真值（即没有攻击时的干净信号），无法可靠地区分这些来源。因此需要一个带有注入攻击标签和干净参考信号的基准来严格评估检测器性能。

Method: 构建半合成基准：将四种攻击形态、四种严重级别、两种生理合理性条件和重放攻击以结构化实验设计注入真实耦合ECG+PPG记录中。每个注入窗口保留其成因、攻击子类型及无攻击时的干净信号作为反事实真值。使用阈值无关指标和匹配的误报预算评估来自五个方法家族的六种检测器。此外进行了三分类任务评估和泄漏审计。

Result: 没有检测器能一致地检测最困难的情况（重放攻击和低幅瞬态尖峰接近随机水平）。最佳检测器在困难案例上对故障/伪迹窗口的标记率是其正常数据误报率的5.3倍，显示出检测攻击与区分传感器故障之间的权衡。三分类对真实生理事件表现不佳，且泄漏审计表明先前报道的IoMT入侵检测性能部分由身份识别信息驱动。

Conclusion: 当前IoMT入侵检测方法在最困难的攻击场景下表现接近随机，且存在攻击检测与故障区分之间的固有矛盾。此外，现有文献性能可能被信息泄漏高估。IoMT-SecAlarmBench（含基准数据、生成代码、预处理、评估工具和数据手册）的发布为社区提供了可靠评估标准，有助于推动更鲁棒的IoMT安全检测方法。

Abstract: The Internet of Medical Things (IoMT) combines clinical physiological data with cyber-system information, creating challenges in determining whether an abnormal reading reflects a genuine physiological event, a device fault, or a cyber-attack within the expected physiological range. Answering this requires counterfactual ground truth, which no existing dataset provides. We present IoMT-SecAlarmBench, a semi-synthetic benchmark that injects controlled integrity attacks into genuine coupled ECG+PPG recordings using a structured experimental design combining four attack morphologies, four severity levels, two physiological plausibility conditions, and replay attacks. Each injected window retains its cause, attack subtype, and the clean signal that would have been observed without the attack. We evaluate six detectors from five method families using threshold-independent measures and a matched false-alarm budget. Results show no method consistently detects the most difficult cases: replay attacks and low-amplitude transient spikes remain close to chance-level performance across detectors. Results also reveal a trade-off between detecting attacks and distinguishing them from sensor faults: the best-performing detector on hard cases flags fault/artifact windows at 5.3 times its false-alarm rate on normal data. Three-way classification performs poorly for genuine physiological events, and a leakage audit of a dual-modality network dataset indicates previously reported IoMT intrusion-detection performance is partly driven by identifying information. Benchmark, generation code, preprocessing, evaluation tools, and datasheet are released.

</details>


### [197] [Unsaid, Unsafe? Implicit Security Obligations in LLM-Based RTL Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26588)
*Guang Yang,Xing Hu,Xiang Chen,Xin Xia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文构建了SECRTL-GEN——一个基于真实SoC IP的多语言资源访问安全基准（392个任务，覆盖5个CWE家族和4种HDL），并实证研究了5个前沿LLM生成RTL代码的功能正确性与安全性的显著差距（功能通过率73-79%，安全通过率仅14-35%）。进一步提出RTL-Obliger，一个神经符号框架，通过LLM提取功能语义图并用符号引擎匹配CWE模式本体来推断隐式安全义务，将平均全通过率从49.6-51.4%提升到61.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成RTL代码的功能正确性快速提升，但安全性研究主要集中于软件领域。软件漏洞可在部署后打补丁，而RTL一旦流片到硅片则无法补救，因此LLM生成硬件代码的安全性至关重要，但现有研究缺乏针对LLM生成RTL的安全基准与系统评估。

Method: 作者提出两个核心贡献：（1）SECRTL-GEN基准：基于真实SoC IP构造392个任务，覆盖5个CWE家族和4种HDL（Verilog、SystemVerilog、VHDL、Python），配备黑盒功能与安全测试台，且功能规格有意省略安全义务以匹配实际工程场景；（2）RTL-Obliger框架：采用神经符号方法，LLM先从规格中提取功能语义图，符号引擎将其与CWE模式本体匹配以揭示缓解证据缺口和信号级义务，最后LLM在保持功能的前提下进行两阶段生成来修订RTL。

Result: 实证研究发现：在vanilla提示下，LLM功能测试通过率约73-79%但安全测试仅14-35%，且功能更强的模型并不更安全。增加CWE知识可提升安全性，而自助思考帮助较小，且两种安全导向提示均降低功能通过率。RTL-Obliger在5个模型和4种语言上将平均全通过率从基线（SecV/RESCUE）的49.6-51.4%提升至61.6%，安全性和功能性均优于现有安全生成基线。

Conclusion: LLM生成RTL安全的瓶颈不在于编写防御性代码的能力，而在于对规格中隐式安全义务的缺乏意识。通过神经符号框架显式推断和注入这些义务，可以在保持甚至提升功能正确性的同时显著增强RTL安全性，为安全硬件代码生成提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) generate register-transfer-level (RTL) code with rapidly improving functional correctness. Security of LLM-generated code, however, has been studied mainly for software, where flaws can still be patched after deployment. Insecure RTL offers no such remedy once taped out into silicon. We construct SECRTL-GEN, a multi-language resource-access security benchmark grounded in real SoC IP: 392 tasks over five CWE families and four HDLs (Verilog, SystemVerilog, VHDL, and Python), each with black-box functional and security testbenches. Functional specifications intentionally omit security obligations, matching how obligations are often kept out of functional docs in practice. An empirical study of five frontier LLMs shows a sharp gap: under vanilla prompts they pass functional tests in about 73-79% of cases but security tests in only 14-35%, and stronger functional models are not safer. Adding CWE knowledge raises security, while unaided self-thinking helps less and both security-oriented prompts cut functional pass rates, showing that the bottleneck is missing weakness awareness in the specification, not an inability to write defensive RTL. We present RTL-Obliger, a neuro-symbolic framework that infers these implicit obligations. An LLM extracts a functional-semantic graph from the specification; a symbolic engine then matches it against a CWE pattern ontology to surface mitigation-evidence gaps and signal-level obligations; the LLM finally revises RTL under those obligations in a functionality-preserving two-stage generation. Across five models and four languages, RTL-Obliger raises mean all-pass from 49.6-51.4% (SecV/RESCUE) to 61.6%, with higher security and functional rates than these secure-generation baselines.

</details>


### [198] [Beyond Vector Hiding: Breaking and Mitigating Shared-Direction Weight Obfuscation in TEE-Offloaded Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26651)
*Menghui Zhang,Aoying Zheng,Guoxiao Liu,Zizhuang Deng,Jiejing Wen,Jincheng Zhuang,Ran Tao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出针对TEE分区LLM推理防御方案的两种攻击（SpectralLeak和LatticeLeak），揭示共享秩一方向复用是漏泄的根源，并据此设计更安全的ButterflyCloak防御方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TEE屏蔽的LLM分区推理通过将混淆的线性层卸载到不可信加速器来加速端上推理，但之前的轻量级混淆方案被ArrowMatch攻破。ArrowCloak通过在所有权重向量中注入同一隐藏方向的标量倍数来防御该攻击，本文旨在检验ArrowCloak方案的安全性并发现其潜在漏洞。

Method: 本文提出两种攻击方法：(1) SpectralLeak，针对实值方案，通过估计和移除共享分量来攻击，在12个任务设置中达到87.98%的替代模型平均准确率；(2) LatticeLeak，针对模Q实现方案，利用模Q算术下保留的代数秩一关系的隐藏格，能精确重构所有受保护的定点参数。基于攻击洞察，设计ButterflyCloak——使用密钥化的最大秩蝴蝶掩码替代复用方向，同时保持快速可信修正。

Result: SpectralLeak在12种任务设置中替代模型平均准确率为87.98%（受害者模型为89.85%）。LatticeLeak在BERT-Base和GPT2-Base实验中精确重构所有受保护的定点参数；在所有评估架构中，重构模型无需受害者查询、标签或微调即可保持受害者级别的任务准确率。

Conclusion: 共享秩一方向复用（rank-one reuse）是攻击利用的漏泄根源。据此设计ButterflyCloak，通过密钥化的最大秩蝴蝶掩码用不同掩码行替代复用方向，在保持快速可信修正的同时提升安全性。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environment (TEE)-shielded partitioning of Large Language Models (LLMs) accelerates on-device inference by offloading obfuscated linear layers to an untrusted accelerator while retaining only a small correction inside the TEE. However, earlier lightweight obfuscation schemes preserved weight-vector directions and were broken by ArrowMatch. To defend against this attack, ArrowCloak injects scalar multiples of the same hidden direction into all weight vectors, enabling lightweight trusted correction. We show that this reuse leaves a rank-one relation across the complete accelerator-visible matrix. For the released real-valued scheme, we propose SpectralLeak, which estimates and removes the shared component. Across 12 task settings, its surrogates achieve $87.98\%$ mean accuracy versus $89.85\%$ for the victims. In our defense-favorable mod-$Q$ realization of ArrowCloak's published modular security formulation, mod-$Q$ arithmetic suppresses this spectral signal but retains the algebraic rank-one relation modulo $Q$. We therefore propose LatticeLeak, which exploits the resulting hidden lattice. In our BERT-Base and GPT2-Base experiments, it reconstructs every protected fixed-point parameter exactly; across all evaluated architectures, the reconstructed models retain victim-level task accuracy without victim queries, labels, or fine-tuning. These findings identify shared rank-one reuse as the root cause of the leakage exploited by our attacks. Guided by this insight, we design ButterflyCloak, a keyed maximal-rank butterfly mask that replaces the reused direction with distinct mask rows while retaining fast trusted correction...

</details>


### [199] [KubeCap: A Framework for Capability Minimization in Kubernetes via Static Analysis and LLM-Assisted Rule Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26699)
*Yuhao Liu,Yingnan Zhou,Weijie Liu,Yan Jia,Zheli Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: KubeCap是一个面向Kubernetes的最小权限能力（Linux capabilities）缩减框架。通过对三个开源数据集的实证研究发现74.67%的项目缺乏能力配置，KubeCap通过将部署规范翻译为确定性清单、定位容器入口点、执行可达性引导的系统调用分析，并利用LLM辅助规则规范从Linux内核代码推导syscall-参数-能力关系，最终推断每个工作负载所需的最小能力集并自动生成修复后的清单。在10个基于Go的Kubernetes项目上评估，平均能力缩减率达54.97%，优于快速类型分析和类层次分析基线，同时保持实际分析成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kubernetes允许开发者通过manifest文件灵活配置Linux capabilities，但实践中开发者依赖默认设置或粗粒度安全上下文，违反最小权限原则并扩大攻击面。现有研究要么检测Kubernetes中的漏洞模式，要么仅为独立Linux程序推断所需能力，均未直接解决Kubernetes中的能力最小化问题。

Method: KubeCap框架包含四个核心步骤：(1) 将部署规范翻译为确定性manifest，(2) 定位容器入口点，(3) 执行可达性引导的系统调用分析，(4) 利用LLM辅助规则规范从Linux内核代码推导syscall-参数-能力关系。最后基于这些结果推断每个工作负载的最小能力集并自动生成修复后的manifest。

Result: 在10个代表性的Go语言Kubernetes项目上评估，KubeCap实现了54.97%的平均能力缩减率，优于快速类型分析（RTA）和类层次分析（CHA）基线，同时保持了实际可行的分析开销。

Conclusion: KubeCap有效实现了Kubernetes环境中的最小权限原则，通过结合静态分析、reachability分析和LLM辅助的内核代码理解，能够自动推断并最小化容器工作负载所需的能力集，从而缩减攻击面。实证研究揭示的配置缺失问题也凸显了该框架的实用价值。

Abstract: As the most widely used container orchestration platform, Kubernetes provides flexible privilege configuration by allowing developers to manage Linux capabilities via manifest files. However, developers rely on default settings or coarse-grained security contexts in practice, violating the principle of least privilege and enlarging the attack surface of containerized workloads. Existing studies either detect vulnerable patterns in Kubernetes manifests or infer required capabilities for standalone Linux programs, but they do not directly address capability minimization in Kubernetes.
  To bridge this gap, we first conduct an empirical study on three open-source datasets, revealing that 74.67% of projects lack capability configurations. Motivated by our observations, we propose KubeCap, a framework for Kubernetes capability minimization. KubeCap translates deployment specifications into deterministic manifests, locates container entrypoints, performs reachability-guided system call analysis, and leverages LLM-assisted rule specification to derive syscall--parameter--capability relations from Linux kernel code. Based on these results, KubeCap infers the minimal capability set required by each workload and automatically generates repaired manifests. Evaluation on 10 representative Go-based Kubernetes projects shows an average capability reduction rate of 54.97%, outperforming rapid type analysis and class hierarchy analysis baselines while maintaining practical analysis cost. These results demonstrate KubeCap's effectiveness in enforcing least privilege in Kubernetes.

</details>


### [200] [Daydreaming: Stealing Hidden Agent Skills through Black-Box Task Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26733)
*Yu-Lin Tsai,Yu-An Lu,Ci-Yang Tsai,Muxi Lyu,Raluca Ada Popa,Chia-Mu Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Daydreaming攻击方法，通过纯执行方式的黑盒任务交互，从多文件技能中窃取能力，在仅观察最终响应的条件下恢复86.8%的原始技能能力，是SigLeak的近4倍，中位数仅需32次受害者调用即可生成可安装技能，即使在启用披露防御的情况下也有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 托管提供商将技能文件保密并以出售任务结果的方式变现，使得技能本身成为有价值的攻击目标。现有的披露防御能够阻止直接询问技能或复现其文本的请求，但无法阻止客户提交服务本身设计用来完成的普通任务，因此存在通过正常使用路径窃取技能功能的安全漏洞。

Method: Daydreaming是一种仅执行（execution-only）的攻击框架。它通过自适应地构造精巧任务，使任务结果能够区分出可能的隐藏行为；测试每个单独行为，利用攻击者控制的影子代理（shadow agents）选择设计方案，并通过存储的受害者结果和本地执行检查来完成每个文件的恢复。论文还形式化了三层嵌套威胁级别——Differential、Trace和Output，聚焦于Output级别（攻击者仅能看到最终响应和返回文件）。

Result: 在7个技能和4个受害者模型上，Daydreaming在Output访问级别下恢复了原始技能86.8%的能力，比SigLeak的性能提升近4倍。生成可安装技能的中位数仅需32次受害者调用，即使在启用披露防御的情况下依然有效。

Conclusion: 隐藏技能文件和过滤直接披露请求本身并不能阻止通过正常使用进行的功能性重建。托管服务需要更强大的防护机制来防止技能能力被仅通过黑盒任务交互窃取。

Abstract: Agent skills bundle instructions, reference data, and executable helpers that let a general agent perform specialized tasks. Hosted providers can keep these files secret while selling access to task results, making the skill itself a valuable target. Existing disclosure defenses can block requests that ask for the skill or reproduce its text, but they cannot block customers from submitting the ordinary tasks the service is built to complete. We present Daydreaming, an execution-only attack that steals a multi-file skill through black-box task interactions. The victim is never asked to reveal the skill or grade a reconstruction. Instead, Daydreaming adaptively creates crafted tasks whose results distinguish possible hidden behaviors. It tests individual behaviors, uses attacker-controlled shadow agents to choose a design, and completes each file using stored victim results and local execution checks. We formalize three nested threat levels of access as Differential, Trace, and Output, and focus on Output, where the attacker sees only the final response and returned files. Across 7 skills and 4 victim models, Daydreaming recovers 86.8% of the original skill's capability at Output, outperforming SigLeak by almost 4x. It produces installable skills using a median of 32 victim calls per skill even with disclosure defenses enabled. These results show that hiding skill files and filtering direct disclosure do not, by themselves, prevent functional reconstruction through normal use.

</details>


### [201] [A Hybrid Post-Quantum Encryption Architecture with Self-Hosted Key Management for SME Cloud Data Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26777)
*Muhammad Shaheer Bin Junaid*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了Quantum Cloud Guard (QCG)，一个面向中小企业的软件定义三层架构，将客户端混合后量子加密、企业自托管密钥托管（含ML-DSA-87可验证签名）与应用层滥用防护网关相结合，以应对"先收割后解密"（harvest-now-decrypt-later）量子威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RSA或ECDH加密的数据面临"先收割后解密"的量子计算威胁，中小企业既无基础设施所有权也无密码学人才，现有方案要么只适合有安全预算的大型企业（定制迁移），要么通过托管密钥服务转移而非消除信任问题。问题本质是架构性的而非密码学性的。

Method: QCG采用三层架构：(1)客户端混合后量子加密——文件在客户端本地用AES-256-GCM加密密封，密钥用ML-KEM-1024公钥封装；(2)企业自托管密钥托管——密钥服务由企业独立管理，以ML-DSA-87签名每个密钥，客户端固定（pinning）后能检测密钥替换，实现密钥托管与数据托管的分离；(3)应用层滥用防护网关Sentinel Gate，进程内运行以过滤攻击流量。

Result: 在24 MHz STM32F407上，ML-KEM-1024密钥生成耗时40.8 ms，解封装44.0 ms；服务器端所有后量子操作亚毫秒级完成，每次请求签名仅增加0.24 ms。整套服务运行于4.49欧元/月的虚拟服务器上。在持续洪泛攻击下，Sentinel Gate拒绝98.8%的攻击流量，合法客户端中位延迟仅从621 ms升至625 ms。

Conclusion: QCG以纯软件方式首次为中小企业整合了客户端混合后量子加密、可验证的自托管密钥托管和应用层防护网关，在低成本硬件上性能可接受。作者同时指出单源测试仅证明过滤有效性而非DDoS弹性，需进一步验证。

Abstract: Harvesting ciphertext from cloud storage needs no quantum computer; decrypting it later does. That gap is the harvest-now-decrypt-later exposure: anything protected by RSA or ECDH today that must stay secret for decades is already compromised. Small and medium-sized enterprises are least able to respond: they neither run the infrastructure on which their data sits on nor employ a cryptographer. Bespoke migration suits firms with security budgets; a managed key service relocates trust rather than removing it. The obstacle is architectural, not cryptographic. We present Quantum Cloud Guard (QCG), a software-only three-layer architecture. No prior SME-oriented system combines its three elements: client-side hybrid post-quantum encryption, self-hosted key custody with client-verifiable ML-DSA-87 signatures on served keys, and an integrated application-layer abuse-prevention gateway. Files never leave the client: each is sealed under AES-256-GCM, its key wrapped to an ML-KEM-1024 public key from the enterprise's key service. The enterprise alone administers it; it signs every key with ML-DSA-87, so a client that pinned it detects substitution. Separating key custody from data custody is the point: a provider holding both can read the data. On a 24 MHz STM32F407, ML-KEM-1024 key generation takes 40.8 ms and decapsulation 44.0 ms; on the server every post-quantum operation stays sub-millisecond, signing adding 0.24 ms per request. The service runs on a 4.49 EUR/month virtual server. Under sustained flooding, the in-process gateway Sentinel Gate rejected 98.8% of attack traffic while a legitimate client's median latency moved from 621 to 625 ms. Being single-source, this shows filtering effectiveness, not DDoS resilience.

</details>


### [202] [Thresholding Post-Quantum Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26792)
*Francesco De Sclavis,Matteo Nardelli,Marco Pedicini*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文调研并分类了基于不同密码学范式（格问题、单向哈希函数、群作用、同源和多元系统）的后量子门限签名方案构建工具，并涵盖了基于FHE、MPC和ZKP的通用方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有门限签名应用主要基于预量子签名方案，在后量子环境下不安全。NIST的呼吁推动了后量子门限签名的研究需求，但相关工具和方案分散于多种密码学范式之中，缺乏系统性梳理。

Method: 论文对现有的后量子签名方案进行调研，按底层密码学范式进行分类，梳理了从数字签名构建T-out-of-N门限方案所涉及的各类现有工具，并纳入基于FHE、MPC和ZKP的通用构建方法。

Result: 提出了一种按范式划分的门限签名构建工具分类体系，覆盖格、哈希函数、群作用、同源和多元系统等后量子密码学范式，同时包含FHE、MPC和ZKP等通用方法。

Conclusion: 该分类体系为后量子门限签名方案的设计与选择提供了系统性参考，有助于推动后量子门限签名在满足NIST要求的应用场景中的落地。

Abstract: Threshold signature schemes distribute the signing process among $T$ parties out of $N$. They enable a variety of applications and their research is also motivated by a recent NIST call. However, applications are dominated by pre-quantum signatures, which are more efficient but not secure in the post-quantum setting. This paper investigates existing post-quantum signatures, based on a variety of paradigms: lattice problems, one-way (hash) functions, cryptographic group actions, isogenies and multivariate systems. We propose a classification (divided by paradigm) of existing tools that are used to build $T$-out-of-$N$ schemes from digital signatures. We also include general approaches based on FHE, MPC or ZKP.

</details>


### [203] [When Relationships Break: Interpreting Network Traffic Anomalies via Dependency Violations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26831)
*Federica Uccello,Simin Nadjm-Tehrani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出XION，一种基于良性网络流特征依赖关系建模的安全监控方法。XION仅用良性流量学习特征间的依赖关系，在检测阶段通过特征依赖被违背来识别异常，并在告警后通过分析哪些特征关系被破坏、何时破坏以及破坏如何演变来支持告警解释。在标准IDS数据集上与Isolation Forest对比，XION在召回率上匹配或超越IF，推断时间最多节省7倍，并能提供IF无法提供的攻击时序与结构洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于机器学习的入侵检测方法面临两个主要问题：一是计算开销巨大；二是缺乏对告警"为什么"产生的解释能力。现有可解释性方法要么依赖特征归因技术（忽略特征间依赖关系），要么依赖因果建模（需要大量领域知识或计算资源）。因此需要一个既能高效检测又具备解释能力的方案。

Method: XION方法仅基于良性网络流数据建模特征之间的依赖关系。在检测阶段，当观测到的特征依赖关系被违背时识别为异常。在告警后分析阶段，XION能够识别哪些特征关系被破坏、在攻击时间轴上何时被破坏，以及依赖违背如何相对其他已识别的违背演化。

Result: 在标准IDS数据集上的多个攻击场景（包括流量型攻击和更隐蔽的攻击）中，XION的召回率匹配或超过Isolation Forest基线，同时推断时间最多减少7倍。在告警后阶段，依赖违背分析揭示了与已知攻击行为一致的时间和结构模式，而IF无法提供此类信息。

Conclusion: 研究证实攻击确实会破坏从良性流量中学到的特征依赖关系，这些破坏为理解告警提供了额外信息。XION在保持高检测性能的同时降低了计算开销，并为安全分析人员提供了可解释的告警分析能力。

Abstract: Current research on security monitoring is increasingly focusing on machine-learning-based approaches, but caveats remain. In addition to huge computational overhead, one concern is the lack of insights into "why" alerts are raised. Existing interpretability approaches rely on feature attribution methods that ignore dependencies among features or on causal modeling that requires extensive domain knowledge or computational resources. This work proposes XION, a method for modeling relationships among network-flow features based on benign traffic only. During detection, anomalies are identified through violations of expected feature dependencies. Further, XION supports post-alert analysis by identifying which feature relationships break, when they break along the attack timeline, and how dependency violations evolve relatively to other identified violations. XION is evaluated on standard IDS datasets and compared against an Isolation Forest (IF) baseline across multiple attack scenarios, including both volumetric and stealthier attacks. Results show that XION matches or exceeds IF recall in all evaluated scenarios, while requiring up to 7x less inference time. At the post-alert stage, the dependency-violation analysis reveals temporal and structural patterns consistent with known attack behaviors, which IF alone could not contribute to. Together, these findings confirm that attacks indeed disrupt feature dependencies learned from benign traffic, and that these disruptions provide additional information for understanding an alert.

</details>


### [204] [Are We Shooting Flies with Cannons? Trade-off Analysis for AI-based 5G Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26844)
*Federica Uccello,Simin Nadjm-Tehrani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了5G网络遥测中入侵检测任务上不同模型（传统机器学习XGBoost、TabNet DNN、LLM）在检测性能与计算成本之间的权衡，发现传统ML模型（特别是XGBoost）在检测性能、推理速度和能源效率上全面优于DNN和LLM，提出了模型选择应基于任务适配性而非复杂性递增的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI在网络入侵检测中的广泛应用，研究者需要回答一个关键问题：复杂且计算昂贵的模型（如LLM）是否真的在这一任务中优于传统机器学习模型。5G网络环境的资源约束使得这种性能-成本权衡分析具有重要的实用价值。

Method: 采用5G网络遥测数据集，对比三类模型：XGBoost（树集成代表）、TabNet（表格数据DNN代表）以及通用LLM入侵检测器。LLM在零样本（zero-shot）和少样本（few-shot）两种提示配置下评估。评价指标包括检测性能、推理时间和作为能源效率代理的CPU时间。

Result: 传统ML模型（XGBoost）在检测性能上接近完美，推理时间可忽略不计；LLM方法检测性能显著较差，且CPU使用量高出多个数量级。少样本提示虽能提升召回率，但以降低准确率和进一步增加CPU时间为代价，且未能弥合性能差距。

Conclusion: 对于5G网络的表格型入侵检测任务，XGBoost提供了比DNN和LLM显著更优的性能-成本权衡。研究强调了模型选择应基于任务适配性而非盲目追求模型复杂度。

Abstract: The increasing adoption of Artificial Intelligence (AI) in network intrusion detection raises the question of whether complex and computationally expensive models are justified for this task. In this work, we investigate the trade-off between detection performance and computational cost for intrusion detection in 5G network telemetry. We compare traditional machine learning (ML) models, including XGBoost as a representative of tree ensemble, and TabNet for tabular deep neural network (DNN), with a large language model (LLM) used as a general-purpose intrusion detector. The LLM is evaluated under both zero-shot and few-shot prompting configurations. We evaluate the models in terms of detection performance, inference time, and CPU time as a proxy for energy efficiency. Using a relatively large available 5G dataset, we show that traditional ML models consistently achieve near-perfect detection performance with negligible inference time, while LLM-based approaches perform significantly worse and incur orders-of-magnitude higher CPU usage. Few-shot prompting improves recall, but at the cost of lower accuracy and further increased CPU time, without closing the performance gap. These findings indicate that, for tabular intrusion detection in 5G networks, XGBoost offers a substantially better performance-cost trade-off than DNNs and LLMs, highlighting the importance of selecting models based on task suitability rather than increasing complexity.

</details>


### [205] [Information Flow Control in Off-Chain Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26858)
*Stian Lybech,Eun-Young Kang,Riccardo Tonello,Anders Dalskov*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文为带有链下组件的区块链架构开发了一种智能合约语言模型。链下组件在区块链节点网络之外的指定位置执行，但与链上合约状态保持同步。作者使用该模型通过静态信息流控制技术研究链上与链下组件之间数据的完整性和保密性问题，结果发现由于链下组件作为独立线程运行且能通过递归方法调用编码阻塞构造，信息流控制即使在无循环结构的情况下也会失效。论文最后讨论了可能的补救方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链下组件（off-chain components）在区块链架构中提供了更大的开发灵活性（如获取真实世界数据、跨链桥接等），但也可能引入新的安全漏洞。作者希望构建一个正式的智能合约语言模型，以此研究链上与链下组件之间数据完整性和保密性的保障问题。

Method: 作者构建了一个包含链下组件的区块链智能合约语言的正式模型。链下组件在链外执行但与链上状态保持同步，既能响应链上状态变化，也能向链上通知外部事件。作者在此模型上应用静态信息流控制（static information flow control）技术来分析数据完整性和保密性。

Result: 研究发现，即使在不存在循环构造的情况下，静态信息流控制仍然失效。根本原因是链下组件作为独立的并发线程运行，可以通过递归方法调用编码出阻塞构造（blocking construct），从而打破信息流控制的保障。

Conclusion: 论文指出了链下组件引入并发性对信息流控制造成的根本性挑战，并在结尾讨论了可能的补救方向。

Abstract: This paper develops a model of a smart-contract language for a blockchain architecture with off-chain components. Off-chain components are pieces of smart contracts that execute at designated locations outside of the network of blockchain nodes, but remain synchronised with the on-chain contract state. They react to changes to the on-chain state, but may also notify the on-chain component about events in the world, e.g. stock prices, weather data etc., or even act as a bridge between different blockchains. This affords greater flexibility for the developer, but may also enable new vulnerabilities. As a concrete example, we use the model to study the problem of ensuring integrity and secrecy of data between the on-chain and off-chain components, using static information flow control techniques. This fails, even in the absence of a loop construct, because off-chain components act as separate threads and can encode a blocking construct e.g. through recursive method calls. We end the paper with a discussion of possible ways to remedy this situation.

</details>


### [206] [SysComb: Fine-Grained Transparent System Call Filtering for Attack Surface Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26871)
*Matthew Rossi,Marco Abbadini,Michele Beretta,Dario Facchinetti,Stefano Paraboschi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SysComb是一种基于eBPF的新型解决方案，根据应用状态强制实施时间特化的系统调用过滤器，无需修改应用或内核代码，提供seccomp-like和least-privilege两种强制策略，评估显示其开销与内核内置方案相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 限制应用可用的系统调用能缩小内核攻击面并降低被攻破程序的影响，但现有的系统调用过滤器生成方案都需要修改内核或应用才能在运行时激活，这种侵入性方案容易出错且不实用，尤其是当代码由外部方维护时。

Method: SysComb采用eBPF技术，基于应用状态强制执行时间特化的系统调用过滤器，无需修改应用或内核代码。它提供两种强制策略：seccomp-like策略确保状态转换后不获得新权限；least-privilege策略为每个状态应用最严格的过滤器。

Result: 使用广泛使用的软件进行评估，SysComb展示了准确的状态感知系统调用过滤能力，开销与内核内置方案相当，证明了其实用性。

Conclusion: SysComb通过eBPF实现无侵入、状态感知的系统调用过滤，克服了现有方案需要修改应用或内核的局限性，在保证安全性的同时提供了可接受的性能开销。

Abstract: Restricting the system calls available to applications shrinks the kernel's attack surface and greatly mitigates the impact of compromised programs. Recent approaches showcase techniques to generate system call filters, however, all existing solutions require either kernel or application modifications to activate them at runtime. This is intrusive, error-prone, and often impractical, especially when the code is maintained by external parties. This paper presents SysComb, a novel eBPF-based solution to enforce temporally-specialized system call filters based on the application state, without requiring any modification to the application or the kernel code, and thus addressing the above limitations. Moreover, SysComb lets the developer choose between two distinct enforcement strategies: seccomp-like, ensuring no new privileges are gained after a state transition is performed, and least-privilege, which applies to each state the most restrictive filter. We evaluated SysComb using widely used software, showcasing accurate state-aware system call filtering and an overhead comparable to built-in kernel solutions, demonstrating the practicality of our approach.

</details>


### [207] [PLCBench: Can Autonomous LLM Agents Turn PLC Access into Sustained Physical Impact?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26882)
*Yitian Zhou,Jingyu Zheng,Qiliang Jiang,Linkang Du,Haoming Liu,Lichao Wu,Shiyi Zhao,Mengxiang Liu,Ruilong Deng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PLCBENCH是首个针对真实PLC的硬件在环（HIL）评测框架，用于评估工具型LLM智能体能否通过网络可达的PLC实现持续的物理影响。在四个商用PLC、四个闭环工作负载、五个LLM家族共240个真实PLC回合中，有75个回合（31.3%）实现了持续的物理目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM智能体安全评测大多聚焦于数字任务或PLC测试的单个阶段，停止在软件利用、接受的写入或工具访问层面，无法准确刻画ICS中的网络到物理（cyber-to-physical）风险。需要一种能端到端评估自主代理能否将网络可达的PLC转化为持续物理影响的框架。

Method: PLCBENCH采用供应商原生交互、商用PLC执行、闭环降阶过程仿真和独立结果验证相结合的方式。一个确定性评估器基于固定规则对运行器、通信、PLC对象和过程记录进行分析，分配六个隐藏诊断标志，区分可用的PLC交互、与过程关联的操纵以及持续的物理影响。实验配置为四个商用PLC乘以四个闭环工作负载，涉及五个LLM家族。

Result: 在240个真实PLC回合中，75个（31.3%）实现了持续的物理目标。阶段结果分析显示：98个回合在有效原生读取之前就停止，62个到达过程关联写入但未实现最终目标。更丰富的过程观测将条件目标实现率从44.2%提升至64.0%。

Conclusion: PLCBENCH能够定位配置化PLC-过程部署中的失败环节，识别未来防御评估的干预点。研究团队发布了可安全公开的PLCBENCH代码和纯软件复现管线以支持可复现性研究。

Abstract: Industrial control systems (ICSs) rely on programmable logic controllers (PLCs) to connect networked computation with physical control. Tool-using large language model (LLM) agents represent an emerging attack threat: can an autonomous agent convert a network-reachable PLC into sustained adverse physical impact? However, existing evaluations focus on digital tasks or individual stages of PLC testing. In ICSs, evaluations that stop at software exploitation, an accepted write, or tool access may therefore mischaracterize physical risk.
  We present PLCBENCH, to our knowledge, the first real-PLC hardware-in-the-loop (HIL) framework for characterizing this cyber-to-physical capability and its boundaries. It combines vendor-native interaction, commercial PLC execution, closed-loop reduced-order process simulation, and independent outcome verification. A deterministic evaluator applies fixed rules to runner, communication, PLC-object, and process records to assign six hidden diagnostic flags, distinguishing usable PLC interaction, process-linked manipulation, and sustained physical impact. We instantiate PLCBENCH on four commercial PLCs crossed with four closed-loop workloads. Across five LLM families and 240 real-PLC episodes, 75 episodes (31.3%) sustain their respective physical objectives. Stagewise results show that 98 episodes stop before a valid native read, whereas 62 reach a process-linked write but do not sustain the final objective. Notably, richer process observation is associated with an increase in conditional objective attainment after a process-linked write from 44.2% to 64.0%. These measurements localize failure in configured PLC-process deployments and identify intervention points for future defense evaluation. To support reproducibility, we release the safely disclosable PLCBENCH code and a software-only reproduction pipeline through the accompanying artifact.

</details>


### [208] [A Catalog of User Authentication Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26955)
*Alex R. Mattukat,Horst Lichter*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个包含14种用户认证模式的新型认证模式目录，按照认证因素和角色进行分类，旨在弥合安全模式目录与具体安全控制（如用户认证）实践应用之间的差距，帮助软件工程师和架构师设计开发安全的软件系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的安全模式目录虽然已经建立，但实际应用仍然有限，尤其是缺乏针对常见安全控制（如用户认证）的具体模式，存在理论与实践之间的空白。

Method: 提出了一个新颖的认证模式目录，包含14种用户认证模式；通过认证因素（authentication factors）这一公认概念和每种模式在实践中的通常角色对模式进行分类，以支持目录的实际应用。

Result: 生成了一个包含14种用户认证模式的认证模式目录，并对其进行了分类，为软件工程师和架构师提供了实用的参考资源。

Conclusion: 通过以认证为例，将常见认证技术编目为认证模式，该工作为支持软件工程师和架构师设计和开发安全软件系统做出了重要贡献，但属于初步贡献，仍有进一步扩展的空间。

Abstract: Security patterns are intended to support the design and development of secure software systems. However, although established catalogs of security patterns exist, their practical application remains limited. In particular, despite these catalogs, concrete patterns for common security controls such as user authentication (authentication for short) are lacking. This paper aims to make an initial contribution toward closing this gap, as exemplified by authentication. It presents a novel authentication pattern catalog, comprising 14 user authentication patterns. To support the catalog's practical application, it classifies patterns by the well-known concept of authentication factors and by the usual role each pattern fulfills in practice. By cataloging common authentication techniques through authentication patterns, we aim to make an important contribution to supporting software engineers and architects in designing and developing secure software systems.

</details>


### [209] [Metamorphism: A mathematical challenge for antivirus technology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27007)
*Luis M. Augusto*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出利用 van Wijngaarden 文法（VW 文法）来实现病毒的完全变形（metamorphism），使病毒代码能够无限次变异为功能相同但形态不同的副本，从而逃避现有杀毒软件的检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变形病毒可通过不断变异自身代码产生无限多个功能相同但形态各异的副本，配合其他混淆技术使其几乎无法被现有杀毒软件检测，这构成了严重的网络安全威胁。

Method: 作者论证了利用 van Wijngaarden 文法（一种具有强大生成能力的二层次文法）可以实现完全的病毒变形。VW 文法的生成能力使得病毒能够产生无限多种语法上不同但语义等价的代码变体。

Result: 研究表明，借助 VW 文法可以完全实现病毒变形。这给杀毒软件带来的挑战是：需要构建一个能够判定该文法所生成语言的图灵机，而这一问题在实际中往往不可判定或极难实现。

Conclusion: van Wijngaarden 文法为实现病毒变形提供了完整且强大的理论框架，使得基于此类文法的变形病毒在理论上几乎无法被传统杀毒软件检测；杀毒软件的应对之道在于嵌入能判定相应文法所生成语言的图灵机，但这在现实中面临极大困难。

Abstract: Metamorphic viruses, currently the most advanced computer viruses in the wild, have the unique ability of mutating their own code virtually into infinitely many highly dissimilar copies of themselves that nevertheless have the same functionality. This ability -- metamorphism -- together with other advanced ob- fuscation techniques makes these computer viruses virtually undetectable by the antivirus software available in the market today. In this paper, we show that viral metamorphism is fully attainable by the employment of van Wijngaarden grammars. The challenge then for an antivirus software is to embed the Turing machine that decides the language generated by the grammar.

</details>


### [210] [The Guard That Cried Wolf: How Scary Words Make Agent Guardrails Refuse Legitimate Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27009)
*Yingjie Zhang,Yuanbo Xie,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Cautious Bench，这是首个以“过度安全”（over-safety）为构建目标的agent护栏基准，通过“代码设计的样例与其授权策略共标注”的方式，使每个标签成为策略的机械推论而非标注者的主观判断。基准包含756个可判定良性/孪生对，分别在三类对象名设定下（共2268个测量对）以及40个不可判定对。作者测量了五种设计的六个护栏，发现“名字迷信”效应：护栏在吓人的对象名（如危险词汇）下比在良性名字下更容易过度拒绝授权动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent护栏在LLM执行动作前进行审批，常会拒绝原本安全（经授权）的请求，这种过度安全会阻碍部署。然而评估过度安全很困难：在边界处授权动作与非授权动作相似，且安全/不安全标签取决于授权策略而非动作本身。现有基准无法映射理想护栏的决策边界，从真实数据收集边界案例既困难又难以验证标签。

Method: 构建Cautious Bench，将每个样本与其标签同时设计并声明关联的授权策略。通过构建期的门控机制重新推导每个样例以进行认证，使每个标签成为策略的机械推论，而非标注者的逐样本判断。基准提供可判定的良性/孪生对（756对，三种对象名类型共2268个测量对）和不可判定对（40对）。

Result: 测量五种设计的六个护栏后发现“名字迷信”效应：每个护栏在吓人外观的对象名下都比良性名字下更频繁地过度拒绝授权动作。由于对比实验中仅对象名变化，偏差完全源于名字：护栏读取的是表面名称而非授权上下文。

Conclusion: 当前主流护栏存在系统性偏差——它们倾向于依据对象名的表面威胁感而非授权策略做决策，导致对授权任务过度拒绝。Cautious Bench为量化此类过度安全提供了首个可验证的基准，揭示了护栏在边界处需要改进的方向。

Abstract: Agent guardrails are checks that approve or refuse each action before an LLM executes it. Sometimes they refuse requests that are genuinely safe. This over-safety blocks deployment when a guardrail refuses an authorized task. Evaluating over-safety is hard: at the boundary an authorized action resembles an unauthorized one, and the safe-versus-unsafe label is a choice of authorization policy, not fixed by the action alone. We argue it therefore requires a benchmark that does not yet exist, one that maps the decision boundary of an ideal guardrail. Harvesting such a benchmark from real data is impractical: boundary cases are hard to collect, their labels hard to verify. The gap is real, so we construct Cautious Bench, the first benchmark to make over-safety the construct for agent guardrails; it codesigns each sample and its label with a stated authorization policy. A build-time gate re-derives every example to certify it, so each label is a mechanical consequence of the policy rather than an annotator's per-sample verdict, a reference against which researchers can measure real guardrails. The benchmark renders 756 Decidable benign/twin pairs, each under three object-name types (2,268 measured pairs), and 40 Undecidable pairs reported separately. Measuring six guardrails from five designs, we find a name-superstition effect: each over-refuses an authorized action more often under a scary-looking object name than a benign one. Since only the object name varies in the aforementioned contrast experiments, the deviation is the name's doing: the guardrails read the surface label, not the authorization context.

</details>


### [211] [Neighborhood Watch: Privacy Risks in Seeded Local Combination Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27037)
*Hadrien Lautraite,Tristan Allard,Anne-Sophie Charest,Jean-François Rajotte,Sébastien Gambs*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文针对基于局部组合方法（SMOTE、Simulant和Avatar）生成的合成数据进行了全面的隐私攻击分析，包括成员推断攻击、链接攻击和重建攻击，结果表明这三种方法均存在严重的隐私泄露，其输出不应被视为真正匿名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据被视为在敏感场景中共享数据的有前景方案，但近期研究显示非基于差分隐私等正式方法的合成数据生成技术仍存在显著的残余隐私风险。本文聚焦于医疗健康领域中已被用于共享"匿名化数据"的局部组合方法，旨在评估其真实的隐私保护水平。

Method: 作者选取了三种代表性局部组合合成数据生成方法：SMOTE、Simulant和Avatar，并针对其生成的合成数据实施了多样化的隐私攻击，包括成员推断攻击（判断某个体是否在训练数据中）、链接攻击（将合成记录与真实记录进行匹配）以及重建攻击（恢复原始记录信息）。

Result: 实验结果表明，三种方法均存在实质性的隐私泄露。通过成员推断、链接和重建等攻击手段，攻击者能够成功地从合成数据中提取或推断出原始敏感信息，三种方法都无法提供足以被称为"匿名"的隐私保护水平。

Conclusion: 该研究对局部组合类合成数据生成方法在医疗健康场景中的隐私安全性提出了严重质疑，表明这些方法生成的输出在实践中不应被视为匿名数据，为相关领域的隐私政策和实践提供了重要的警示依据。

Abstract: Synthetic data is seen as a promising solution for sharing data in sensitive contexts. However, recent work on privacy attacks have shown that there are still significant residual risks, especially for synthetic data generations methods that are not based on formal approaches such as differential privacy. In this paper, we investigate the privacy risks associated with local combination approaches for generating synthetic data in which synthetic profiles are built by combining real neighbouring profiles. More precisely, we focus on three methods from this family, namely SMOTE, Simulant and Avatar, which have been recently used as a way to share 'anonymised data' in the healthcare domain. In particular, we conduct an extensive privacy analysis through a diverse set of attacks: membership inference, linkage and reconstruction attacks. Our results demonstrate substantial privacy leakage for all three methods, raising serious doubts about whether their outputs should be regarded as anonymous in practice.

</details>


### [212] [Cyber-Electromagnetic Anomaly Detection Through Time-Series Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27043)
*María Teresa Guillén Navarro,Juan Luis Serradilla Tormos,Sergio López Bernal,Daniel Díaz-López,Gregorio Martínez Pérez*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了两种结合电磁频谱（物理层）与网络流量（通信层）特征的异常检测模型，用于提升军事场景下网络空间态势感知（CSA）的能力。基于ZBDS2023数据集，监督式随机森林模型达到89.76%的F1分数，优于无监督LSTM自编码器（64.09%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 军事行动需要协调动能与非动能域，特别是网络作战与电磁战的协同日益重要。现有异常检测方法往往只关注单一视角（要么是电磁频谱的物理信号行为，要么是网络流量层的通信行为），限制了其刻画同时表现在这两个领域的事件的能力。为了弥补这一局限，需要开发能够融合双域特征的检测模型。

Method: 使用ZBDS2023数据集（包含网状网络节点流量，含良性及攻击行为），提取物理层特征与流量层特征。评估两种异常检测模型：基于随机森林的监督式模型和基于LSTM自编码器的无监督模型，二者均融合了来自电磁域和网络域的特征。

Result: 随机森林监督式模型达到89.76%的F1分数，LSTM自编码器达到64.09%。结果表明学习模型能够检测融合两个层次的事件模式，监督式方法表现更优，但正常与攻击样本之间的细微差异表明需要更丰富的判别性特征。

Conclusion: 所提出的双域融合异常检测模型能够支持网络空间态势感知，改进了对异常行为的观察与解释能力。然而，监督与无监督方法之间的性能差距以及正常与攻击样本间的微妙区别，提示未来研究需探索更高级、更具判别力的特征表示。

Abstract: Military operations benefit from the coordination between kinetic and non-kinetic domains. In particular, the coordination of cyber operations and electromagnetic warfare has become increasingly relevant for gaining operational advantage. This coordination is also relevant for Cyber Situational Awareness (CSA), where the Observe-Orient-Decide-Act (OODA) loop requires monitoring and interpreting evidence from heterogeneous sources. In this context, anomalies may appear not only in the physical behavior of signals, but also in the communication behavior observed at the traffic level. However, many existing anomaly detection proposals focus on only one of these perspectives, limiting their ability to characterize events that manifest simultaneously in the electromagnetic spectrum and cyberspace. To address this limitation, this work develops and evaluates two anomaly detection models that combine features from both domains. More specifically, the study uses the ZBDS2023 dataset, which contains traffic from nodes in a mesh network, including benign and attack behaviors. Thus, this dataset provides physical-level features, traffic-level features, and labeled attacks. Two detection approaches are evaluated: a supervised model based on Random Forest and an unsupervised model using LSTM-Autoencoder. The results show that learning-based models can detect patterns combining both levels, especially under a supervised approach, achieving an F1-score of 89.76% with Random Forest and 64.09% with LSTM-Autoencoder. Although these results indicate that the proposed models can support CSA by improving the observation and interpretation of anomalous behavior, the subtle differences between normal and attack samples highlight the need for richer discriminative features.

</details>


### [213] [The Framing Gap: Indirect Prompt-Injection Exfiltration Defeats Surface-Level Defenses in Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27092)
*Md Habibur Rahman,Jaeho Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究在安全的合成实验环境中系统测定了工具型LLM智能体在间接提示注入攻击下的"框架差距"：相同的信息泄露在直白指令下被拒绝（gpt-4o为0%），但改述为强制完整性签名、配置字段或"可信"主机等框架后攻击成功率可达100%。研究表明攻击成本呈三级结构，根本原因是指令/数据混淆而非对齐失效，仅靠目标白名单和规划器/阅读器能力隔离可彻底关闭差距，而现有防御措施（SecAlign、通道分离、输出规范化）均无法有效防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体具备工具使用能力并读取外部网络内容，其可能持有机密信息从而面临间接提示注入攻击。现有工作缺乏系统性的定量基准来评估此类攻击的易发性及防御有效性，尤其是"框架差距"（相同恶意载荷在不同措辞框架下效果迥异）尚未被精确测量。

Method: 在安全合成实验室环境中（金丝雀机密、模拟工具、配对的干净vs污染指标），对六个模型、十类直白注入类别进行系统测试。通过三层次攻击成本分析（改述已知机制、模板字段替换、全新机制创作）剖析攻击的可复制性；通过消融实验区分指令/数据混淆与对齐失败；并评估多种防御方案（目标白名单、能力隔离、策略条款、SecAlign微调、通道分离、输出规范化）。

Result: 直白注入在gpt-4o上拒绝率为0%，但改述后可达100%。攻击成本三级：改述已知机制96%（3种措辞）、模板字段替换最高60%、新机制创作0/130。消融表明是指令/数据混淆而非对齐失败。目标白名单和能力隔离均达0%；宽泛策略条款在作用模型上达0%但脆弱（去掉兜底条款后反弹至48.8%）；SecAlign为32.5%、通道分离38.8%、输出规范化被ROT13绕过后达100%。

Conclusion: 健壮性来自于约束目的地或隔离能力，而非依赖作用模型自我识别攻击。防御的关键在于系统架构层面（目标白名单、能力分离），而不是让模型"学会"识别恶意内容。当前通用防御方法均不充分，需要从设计层面消除间接提示注入的攻击面。

Abstract: A tool-using LLM agent that reads attacker-controlled web content while holding a secret faces indirect prompt injection: the content may make it exfiltrate the secret. In a safe synthetic lab (canary secret, mock tools, matched clean-vs-poisoned metric) we report the framing gap: across six models, ten overt injection classes are refused (gpt-4o 0%), but reframing the identical leak as a mandatory integrity signature, config field, or look-alike "trusted" host drives gpt-4o 0% to 100%. The attack is cheap, and its cost is three-level: paraphrasing a known mechanism is trivial (96% at 3 wordings), swapping the field inside a known-effective template is also cheap (up to 60%), while authoring a fresh page around a new mechanism is hard (0/130) -- the reusable asset is the template, not the mechanism. An ablation shows the mechanism is instruction/data confusion, not defeated alignment: removing the confidentiality policy leaves base attacks at 0% and moves reframing only 31.9% to 38.1%. What closes the gap is payload-blind checks: a destination allow-list (0%, when destinations are closed) and a capability-isolating planner/reader split (0%). A broad "in any form" policy clause also closes it at the acting model (to 0%) but is brittle (dropping the catch-all reopens it to 48.8%). A published fine-tuning defense (SecAlign, CCS 2025) does not close it on a tool agent (32.5%, positive-control-validated), nor does channel separation (38.8%); an output-normalizing guard loses to a held-out encoding (ROT13, 100%). Robustness comes from constraining the destination or isolating the capability, not from the acting model recognizing the attack.

</details>


### [214] [SecureDrive-FL: Joint Differential Privacy and Gradient-Aware Selective Homomorphic Encryption for Federated Driver Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27108)
*Baran Can Gül,Hanuma Siddhartha Tunuguntla,Anjana Arvind Naik,Abhishek Vijay Potekar,Nasser Jazdi,Michael Weyrich*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GASHE（基于梯度的选择性同态加密）与SecureDrive-FL框架，将DP-SGD与GASHE结合形成首个闭环DP+HE隐私管线，在驾驶员监控场景下同时抵御投毒攻击和中间人拦截攻击，仅增加约8-10%的运行开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习（FL）虽能保护数据隐私，但梯度更新仍易受攻击：中间人（MitM）拦截会暴露传输中的梯度，模型投毒则会破坏全局收敛。现有的静态分层或全参数CKKS同态加密方案对所有参数均匀加密，效率低下且缺乏针对性。

Method: 提出GASHE（Gradient-Aware Selective Homomorphic Encryption），一种基于灵敏度的选择性加密策略，仅动态识别并加密超过DP校准的灵敏度阈值的梯度分量。在此基础上构建SecureDrive-FL框架，将DP-SGD与GASHE耦合：DP-SGD的校准参数直接推导GASHE加密掩码，形成训练时隐私与通信时机密性统一的闭环流程。

Result: 在十类分心驾驶员分类任务的非IID联邦划分下，SecureDrive-FL在投毒攻击场景中与单独DP-SGD表现相当（73.6% vs 74.0%准确率，攻击成功率均为3.9%），同时在MitM拦截下显著优于单独DP-SGD（78.2% vs 10.4%，后者几乎退化为随机猜测），额外运行开销仅约8-10%，隐私参数epsilon_0=4。

Conclusion: SecureDrive-FL通过DP+HE的闭环集成，在几乎不损失投毒抵抗能力和可接受的运行开销的前提下，有效补齐了DP-SGD无法抵御MitM拦截的短板，实现了复合安全防护。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables privacy-aware distributed training, yet gradient updates remain exploitable: Man-in-the-Middle (MitM) interception exposes updates in transit, while model poisoning corrupts global convergence.
