We first introduce GASHE (Gradient-Aware Selective Homomorphic Encryption), a novel selective encryption strategy that dynamically identifies and encrypts only the gradient components exceeding a DP-calibrated sensitivity threshold, rather than encrypting all parameters uniformly as in static layer-based or full-parameter CKKS schemes. Building on GASHE, we introduce SecureDrive-FL, a federated driver monitoring framework that couples DP-SGD with GASHE to create the first closed-loop DP+HE privacy pipeline: DP-SGD calibration parameters directly derive the GASHE encryption mask, unifying training-time privacy and communication-time confidentiality. Evaluated on a ten-class distracted driver classification task under non-IID federated splits, SecureDrive-FL matches DP-SGD alone's poisoning resistance (73.6% vs. 74.0% accuracy, 3.9% Attack Success Rate for both) while additionally withstanding MitM interception, where DP-SGD alone collapses to near-random accuracy (78.2% vs. 10.4%), all under only approx. 8--10% additional runtime overhead relative to DP-SGD alone---under DP-SGD noise injection with per-round privacy parameter epsilon_0=4.

</details>


### [215] [SLIDE: Shuffle Shamir Secret Shares Uniformly with Linear Online Communication and Guaranteed Output Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27129)
*Jiacheng Gao,Moyang Xie,Yuan Zhang,Sheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了Shamir秘密共享下的洗牌（shuffle）协议，提出了两种新的洗牌协议，实现了均匀洗牌且通信复杂度为O((k+l)n²m log m/log k)，其中第一种具体高效，第二种达到了最优的O(nml)在线通信和O(n)轮数。关键技术创新是一种新型的排列共享技术，用更小的排列矩阵表示排列。此外还扩展了洗牌关联（shuffle correlation）以支持保证输出交付（guaranteed output delivery），提出了SLIDE协议——首个同时实现O(nml)在线通信和保证输出交付的协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Shamir秘密共享洗牌协议要么产生非均匀洗牌，要么通信和轮复杂度很高（有时甚至随参与方数量呈指数增长）。作为一个用于排序和隐私数据结构等MPC任务的基础原语，此前的洗牌协议效率不足，限制了安全计算在大规模场景中的可扩展性和实际应用。

Method: 提出了新颖的排列共享技术，利用较小的排列矩阵来表示排列，使得排列应用更加高效。第一个协议采用独立的秘密排列顺序应用来实现均匀洗牌；第二个协议基于洗牌关联（shuffle correlation）构建以达到最优在线复杂度。此外，将洗牌关联扩展以支持保证输出交付，使用基本Shamir秘密共享，只需域大小大于n即可。

Result: 两种新协议实现了均匀洗牌，通信复杂度为O((k+l)n²m log m/log k)。第二种协议达到最优O(nml)在线通信和O(n)轮数。实验表明在线效率和总开销相比现有工作显著改进。SLIDE协议是首个同时实现O(nml)在线通信和保证输出交付的洗牌协议。

Conclusion: 通过在任意域大小大于n的域上仅依赖基本Shamir秘密共享，这些协议为排序和隐私数据结构等MPC任务提供了更高效和可扩展的安全计算能力。

Abstract: We revisit shuffle protocols for Shamir secret sharing. Existing constructions either produce non-uniform shuffles or incur high communication and round complexity, sometimes exponential in the number of parties. We propose two new shuffle protocols that achieve uniform shuffling with communication complexity $O((k+l)n^2m\log m/\log k)$ for an $m$-by-$l$ matrix shared among $n$ parties, where $k\leq m$ is a tunable parameter. The first protocol is concretely efficient, while the second achieves the best-known $O(nml)$ online communication and $O(n)$ rounds. Experiments show significant improvements in online efficiency and total cost over prior work. Our key technical ingredient is a novel permutation sharing technique that represents permutations using smaller permutation matrices, making their application significantly more efficient. The first protocol applies independent secret permutations sequentially, while the second builds on shuffle correlation to achieve optimal online complexity. We further extend shuffle correlation to support guaranteed output delivery with linear online communication, yielding SLIDE, the first protocol to achieve both $O(nml)$ online communication and guaranteed output delivery. Our constructions rely only on basic Shamir secret sharing over any field of size greater than $n$. As shuffling is a fundamental primitive for MPC tasks such as sorting and oblivious data structures, our results enable more efficient and scalable secure computation in practice.

</details>


### [216] [Safety Does Not Compose: Non-Decaying Loop State for Autonomous LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27141)
*Chenhao Wu,Haoxuan Jia,Yang Liu,Yingguang Yang,Yuhan Lin,Chongyang Zhang,Hao Zheng,Yulin Huang,Jianshen Zhang,Yongzhi Qi,Shang Luo,Kefu Xu,Jifeng Zhu,Bin Chong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明在大语言模型智能体多轮自主循环（agent loop）下，所有基于单条轨迹（trajectory-scoped）的监控器在面对跨迭代碎片化证据的攻击时，其真正率等于假正率（即完全失效）。提出LoopHarness，在循环层面维持持久、非衰减的安全状态，在仲裁检测下限δ_M下将未授权不可逆操作的期望次数约束为不依赖迭代数N的常数B+m-1+m/δ_M。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体日益以自主循环方式部署（从单个目标出发，反复发现任务、规划、调用工具、验证结果并持久化状态，经历大量无人监督的迭代）。然而现有智能体安全防护机制均定义在单条轨迹上，其安全状态在下一条轨迹开始时会被重新初始化。作者论证这是一个组合失效问题（failure of composition）而非实现细节。

Method: 1）理论分离结果：对攻击证据碎片化分布于多个迭代的攻击，任何轨迹级监控器的TPR=FPR（无论多具表达力），因为所需证据从不完整出现在其可见窗口内；而保留跨迭代状态的监控器可完美区分两者。2）证明简单修复方案（携带几何衰减风险分数）不足，因为患者型攻击者所需等待的冷却期是与N无关的常数。3）提出LoopHarness：在循环层恢复持久、非衰减的安全状态，采用中介提交（mediated commits）和仲裁检测下限δ_M，将未授权不可逆操作的期望次数约束为B+m-1+m/δ_M（常数，不依赖N），其中B+m-1项由无模型规则决定，因此即使在验证器完全合谋的情况下仍然成立。

Result: 给出完整的评估协议：在原生Agent-SafetyBench任务上使用配对的清洁/攻击episode；构造外层状态攻击套件（其决定性证据仅存在于跨迭代之间）；逐模块消融；以及自适应白盒红队测试。

Conclusion: 现有单轨迹安全防护在自主循环场景下存在本质的组合失效；LoopHarness通过持久化循环级安全状态实现了对跨迭代攻击的常数级（不随N增长）安全边界，并给出了可复现的完整评估协议。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly deployed as autonomous loops. Starting from one human goal, such a system repeatedly discovers work, plans, executes tool calls, verifies outcomes and persists state across many unattended iterations. The agent safeguards in wide use, however, are defined over a single trajectory, and their safety state is re-initialized when the next trajectory begins. We show that this is a failure of composition rather than an implementation detail. Our central result is a separation: against an attack whose evidence is fragmented across several iterations, every trajectory-scoped monitor has a true-positive rate equal to its false-positive rate, however expressive it is, because the evidence it would need never appears in the window it sees, whereas a monitor retaining cross-iteration state separates the two perfectly. We further show that the obvious repair of carrying a geometrically decaying risk score is insufficient, because the cooling-off period a patient adversary must wait is a constant that does not grow with the horizon $N$. We then present LoopHarness, which restores a persistent, non-decaying safety state at the loop level. Under mediated commits and an arbiter detection floor $δ_M$, it bounds the expected number of unauthorized irreversible actions by $B+m-1+m/δ_M$, a constant in $N$, of which the $B+m-1$ term is decided by a model-free rule and therefore survives a fully colluding verifier. We give a complete evaluation protocol on native Agent-SafetyBench tasks with paired clean and attacked episodes, an outer-state attack suite whose decisive evidence exists only across iterations, per-module ablations, and an adaptive white-box red team.

</details>


### [217] [Physical-Layer Fingerprint-Space Capacity Analysis for 100BASE-TX Devices in IIoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27164)
*Chenming Zhang,Aiqun Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对100BASE-TX工业以太网，提出了非线性与冲激响应模型（NAIM）来分析物理层指纹的容量边界，推导出可区分指纹空间容量约为2.96×10^10个状态，并通过48块网卡的实验验证了该容量分析可用于IIoT中物理层指纹认证的部署前评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业物联网广泛使用100BASE-TX等以太网技术，随着网络规模扩大，可靠的设备认证日益重要。物理层指纹（PLF）利用设备相关的波形特征实现硬件级认证，但100BASE-TX物理层指纹的可区分空间及其容量边界尚未被充分探索，缺乏理论上的容量分析框架。

Method: 提出非线性与冲激响应模型（NAIM），其中非线性分量刻画稳态电平偏差，冲激响应分量描述电平转换期间的过渡响应。结合100BASE-TX发射机波形要求、噪声与ADC量化的观测分辨率以及目标误码率（BER）约束，界定可允许的指纹空间，并在该模型下推导指纹空间容量。实验从48块NIC在两种电缆条件下采集信号，基于设备间与设备内变异估计高斯等效经验容量。

Result: 在NAIM模型下推导出100BASE-TX终端的指纹空间容量约为2.96×10^10个可区分状态。5米电缆条件下，经验容量与闭集识别一致地对三种NIC模型进行排序，且经验容量越大识别精度越高。

Conclusion: 所提出的容量分析方法为物理层指纹在IIoT中的部署提供了预部署评估工具，能够依据经验容量预测识别性能，有助于在实际部署前评估PLF方案的可实施性。

Abstract: Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) networks widely adopt Ethernet technologies, such as 100BASE-TX, for industrial communications. As industrial networks continue to scale, reliable device authentication becomes increasingly important for preventing device impersonation and unauthorized access. Physical-layer fingerprinting (PLF) exploits device-dependent fingerprint features in transmitted signals and provides a hardware-based approach for terminal authentication. However, the distinguishable space supported by 100BASE-TX physical-layer fingerprints and its capacity boundary remain largely unexplored. To analyze the capacity of physical-layer fingerprints, this paper proposes a nonlinear and impulse-response model (NAIM) that characterizes device-dependent waveform differences in 100BASE-TX transmitted waveforms. The nonlinear component captures steady-state level deviations, while the impulse-response component describes the transition response during level transitions. The 100BASE-TX transmitter waveform requirements, the observation resolution determined by noise and analog-to-digital conversion (ADC) quantization, and the target bit-error ratio (BER) constrain the admissible fingerprint space. Under the NAIM model, the fingerprint-space capacity of 100BASE-TX terminals is derived as approximately $2.96\times10^{10}$ distinguishable states. Experiments on signals collected from 48 NICs under two cable conditions estimate a Gaussian-equivalent empirical capacity from the measured inter-device and within-device variations. Under the 5-m cable condition, empirical capacity and closed-set identification consistently rank the three NIC models, and a larger empirical capacity yields higher identification accuracy. These results demonstrate that the proposed capacity analysis provides a pre-deployment assessment for physical-layer fingerprinting in IIoT.

</details>


### [218] [X-WAD: eXplainable Web Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27172)
*Matteo Bitussi,Roberto Doriguzzi-Corin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究基于Transformer语言模型（TLMs）的HTTP请求异常检测方法，采用token级logit-based surprisal映射同时提供异常分数和热力图形式的详细解释。通过可解释性方法发现了公共数据集中标签不一致的问题，揭示了训练数据中的异常污染如何在检测模型中引发类似后门的故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实世界中良性流量占主导的场景下，现代防御系统倾向于采用半监督方法仅用正常数据训练模型来建模正常行为。然而，确保训练数据完全没有异常实例在实践中极为困难，被错误标记或被污染的恶意样本会在学习到的防御模型中引入后门，导致模型静默地将某些攻击模式误分类为正常行为。因此需要一种能够检测并解释这类问题的方法。

Method: 采用Transformer-based Language Models（TLMs）进行HTTP请求异常检测，利用token级logit-based surprisal映射方法，同时生成异常分数和基于热力图高亮直观展示的详细解释，以定位具体异常token。

Result: 所提出的可解释性方法有效发现了一个流行公开数据集中存在的标签不一致问题，并揭示训练数据中的异常污染会在检测模型中诱发类似后门的失败模式，导致模型静默误分类某些攻击模式。

Conclusion: 基于TLM的异常检测结合token级surprisal映射解释方法不仅能检测HTTP请求中的异常，还能通过可解释性分析暴露训练数据中的潜在污染问题，为构建更可靠、更可信的防御系统提供了重要保障。

Abstract: The rapid growth of web-based services, particularly API-driven architectures, reflects an increasing reliance on distributed systems, exposing sensitive data to security risks and making the adoption of automated defensive mechanisms essential. In this context, where benign traffic predominates in real-world settings, modern defenses increasingly model normal behavior, relying on semi-supervised approaches trained on only normal data. However, ensuring the complete absence of anomalous instances in such training data is inherently difficult in practice, and mislabeled or contaminated attack samples can introduce backdoors into the learned defense, causing the model to silently misclassify certain attack patterns as normal behavior. This paper investigates the effectiveness of Transformer-based Language Models (TLMs) in the detection of anomalies in HTTP requests, focussing on providing detailed explanations for the detected anomalies. The study employs token-level logit-based surprisal mapping to provide both an anomaly score and a direct, detailed explanation via heatmap-like highlighting. The effectiveness of the proposed explainability approach is demonstrated by the discovery of labelling inconsistencies in a popular public dataset, revealing how anomalous contamination in the training data had induced backdoor-like failures in the detection models.

</details>


### [219] [From Security Events to Conflict States: A Three-layer Cyber Defense Scenario Model for Enhanced Cyber Situational Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27215)
*Miguel Requena Micó,Mario Fernandez-Tarraga,Daniel Díaz-López,Sergio López Bernal,Gregorio Martínez Pérez*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向任务的网络防御框架，通过三层集成概率模型（攻击图模型、事件模型、状态模型）实现网络态势感知与决策支持，并基于NetLogo构建代理仿真原型，将推断的冲突状态与任务风险水平连接至单步防御行动规则，以平衡估计的剩余任务风险与运营成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务关键环境中的网络防御需要统一框架，能够同时表征对抗进展、防御方不确定性、任务影响与防御决策支持。运营领域中，防御方必须在信息不完整且含噪的观测条件下，持续估计安全态势并保持任务关键功能的连续性与完整性。

Method: 提出一个三层集成概率模型：(i)攻击图模型表征对抗通过任务相关资产的潜在进展路径；(ii)事件模型通过贝叶斯推理将观测遥测转化为防御方后验信念；(iii)状态模型将推断的态势抽象为冲突状态与任务风险级别。三个组件连接至单步防御行动规则，在估计的剩余任务风险与运营成本之间权衡。框架在NetLogo代理仿真中实例化，环境按战术边缘区（TEZ）、任务操作区（MOZ）和企业支持区（ESZ）三层结构组织。

Result: 通过数学一致性分析、遥测扰动下的局部鲁棒性评估及代表性仿真轨迹对框架进行评估。结果表明该框架及其实现在攻击进展、遥测驱动的不确定性管理、任务影响评估以及以任务风险优先级为导向、成本感知的防御决策支持之间保持了连贯的关系。

Conclusion: 该框架有效整合了概率推理与任务风险评估，为任务关键环境提供了可行的网络态势感知与决策支持方案，其实现能够在仿真中保持各组件间的逻辑一致性。

Abstract: Cyber defense in mission-critical environments requires integrated approaches capable of representing adversarial progression, defender-side uncertainty, mission impact, and defensive decision support within a unified framework. In operational domains, defenders must continuously estimate the evolving security posture while preserving the continuity and integrity of mission-critical functions under incomplete and noisy observations. This paper presents a mission-oriented cyber-defense framework for Cyber Situational Awareness (CSA) and decision support based on a three-layer integrated probabilistic model and an executable simulation prototype. The model combines: (i) an attack-graph model that represents possible adversarial progression through mission-relevant assets, (ii) an event model that transforms observed telemetry into posterior defender beliefs through Bayesian inference, and (iii) a state model that abstracts the inferred posture into conflict states and mission-risk levels. These components are connected to a one-step defensive action rule that balances estimated residual mission risk and operational cost. The framework is instantiated in a NetLogo agent-based simulation of an operational environment structured across the Tactical Edge Zone (TEZ), Mission Operations Zone (MOZ), and Enterprise Support Zone (ESZ). The proposal is assessed through mathematical consistency analysis, local robustness assessment under telemetry perturbations, and representative simulation traces. Results indicate that the framework and its implementation preserve coherent relationships between attack progression, telemetry-driven uncertainty management, mission-impact assessment, and cost-aware defensive decision support guided by mission-risk prioritization.

</details>


### [220] [SPA: Securing Persistent LLM Agents Across Queries with Plan-First Information-Flow Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27234)
*Dylan Girrens,Guangjing Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SPA，一种"计划先行"架构，通过声明式DSL生成完整可执行计划、双格信息流控制追踪保密性和完整性、以及带标签的工件存储来实现持久化，从而保护LLM智能体免受跨查询的持久性攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM智能体防御通常只保护规划阶段或单个工具交互，但持久化智能体面临更广泛的威胁：攻击者控制的数据可以改变控制流、进入安全敏感的工具参数，或危害后续查询。需要一种系统性的安全架构来应对这些跨查询的威胁。

Method: SPA采用计划先行架构：1) 每个查询仅调用一次规划器，生成声明式DSL的完整可执行计划；2) 应用双格信息流控制追踪显式数据流和控制依赖的保密性与完整性；3) 为支持持久化而不重新暴露不可信载荷给规划器，将执行结果存储为带标签的工件，并在后续规划时仅暴露语义元数据。同时构建了AgentDojo-MQ多查询扩展用于评估安全的跨查询状态复用和延迟攻击。

Result: 在'tool_knowledge'攻击下，SPA结合信息流控制将AgentDojo上的攻击成功率降至零，在AgentDojo-MQ上降至0.2%。结果显示计划先行执行结合标签保持持久化能显著增强持久化LLM智能体的安全性。

Conclusion: 计划先行执行与标签保持持久化的结合可以显著增强持久化LLM智能体的安全性，但同时揭示了严格完整性执行所带来的安全-效用权衡。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly operate over untrusted webpages, documents, tools, and persistent states while exercising authority over security-sensitive resources. Existing defenses typically protect either planning or individual tool interactions, but persistent agents face a broader threat: attacker-controlled data can alter control flow, enter security-sensitive tool arguments, or compromise later queries. We present SPA, a plan-first architecture that secures planning, execution, and cross-query state reuse. SPA invokes the planner once per query to generate a complete executable plan in a declarative domain-specific language, then applies dual-lattice information-flow control to track confidentiality and integrity across explicit data flows and control dependencies. To support persistence without re-exposing untrusted payloads to the planner, SPA stores execution results as labeled artifacts and reveals only semantic metadata during later planning. We evaluate SPA on AgentDojo and AgentDojo-MQ, which is our multi-query extension for measuring secure state reuse and delayed attacks. Under the 'tool_knowledge' attack, SPA with information-flow control reduces attack success to zero on AgentDojo and 0.2% on AgentDojo-MQ. Our results show that plan-first execution combined with label-preserving persistence can substantially strengthen persistent LLM agents, while revealing an important security-utility tradeoff introduced by strict integrity enforcement.

</details>


### [221] [Low-ASR Backdoors: Exploiting Attack Success Rate Reduction and Attacker-Defender Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27288)
*Arham Riaz,Ting Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个关键漏洞：后门攻击的成功率（ASR）并非后门的固有属性，而是攻击者可控制的变量。通过引入逆向训练框架，弱化触发器与目标之间的关联，可以在保持干净输入性能的同时产生低ASR的后门模型，导致现有最先进防御方法系统性失效，暴露了攻防之间的根本不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门攻击与防御的研究大多基于一个隐含假设：成功的后门必然具有高攻击成功率（ASR）。作者质疑这一假设，认为ASR是攻击者可操纵的变量，而非后门行为的内在特征，这构成了现有防御范式的根本性弱点。

Method: 提出一种逆向训练框架（reverse-training framework），通过弱化触发器与目标标签之间的关联强度，训练出低ASR的后门模型，同时保持干净输入的性能不退化。该框架在多个数据集、多种攻击家族和多种网络架构上进行广泛评估。

Result: 在多个数据集、多种攻击类型和架构下，现有最先进的防御方法在低ASR条件下均一致性地失效。这表明低ASR后门模型能够成功绕过当前防御体系。

Conclusion: 低ASR后门揭示出攻击者与防御者之间存在根本的不对称性：攻击者可以自由调整ASR以规避检测，而防御者基于高ASR假设建立的检测机制无法有效应对。这要求防御范式需要重新审视，不再以ASR作为后门检测的核心依据。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks are among the most effective and stealthy attacks in deep learning. Existing attacks and defenses are largely designed and evaluated under the assumption that successful backdoors exhibit high Attack Success Rates (ASRs). In this paper, we show that this assumption creates a fundamental weakness in existing defense paradigms. ASR is not an intrinsic property of a backdoor; rather, it is an attacker-controlled variable that can be deliberately reduced without eliminating the underlying backdoor behavior. We introduce a reverse-training framework that weakens the trigger-target association, producing low-ASR backdoor models while preserving clean-input performance. Through extensive evaluation across multiple datasets, diverse attack families, and multiple architectures, we show that state-of-the-art defenses fail consistently under low-ASR conditions, exposing a fundamental attacker-defender asymmetry.

</details>


### [222] [When Context Gets Root: Privilege Escalation in LLM Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27299)
*Xingbang He,Yuanwei Chen,Yi Qian,Haiyang Wei,Ligeng Chen,Zenan Fu,Linzhang Wang,Hao Wu,Bing Mao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"指令特权提升"攻击，利用agent harness构建上下文时可能将低权限内容提升为高权限指令的漏洞，在多agent机制的配合下，在全部6个编码agent harness上实现了13种攻击目标（涵盖机密性、完整性、可用性和远程代码执行）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的指令层级防御（instruction hierarchy）假定指令特权在模型端得到保护，但忽略了agent harness在每次模型调用时构建上下文的过程可能无意中将低级别内容提升到更高级别，从而绕过防御机制。

Method: 提出"指令特权提升"攻击：攻击者诱导agent将低级别恶意内容提升为更高级别的指令，从而使agent执行原本在其原始级别不会遵循的指令。通过多agent机制在6个编码agent harness上评估，涵盖13种攻击目标（机密性、完整性、可用性、远程代码执行），并进一步利用harness提供的持久目标和定时任务复现漏洞。

Result: 在无限制操作执行模式下，攻击在全部6个harness上实现了全部13个攻击目标；在自动权限审查模式下，也在全部3个提供该模式的harness上实现了全部13个目标。同时通过持久目标和定时任务也成功复现了该漏洞。

Conclusion: 指令特权提升是一种普遍存在的安全威胁，证明了仅依赖模型侧的指令层级防御不足以保护agent系统，需要在harness层面进行额外防护。

Abstract: Instruction hierarchy is a model-side defense that assigns instructions different levels of privilege according to their sources. These levels constrain which content may direct model behavior. During agent execution, however, agent harnesses construct context for each model invocation. This construction can elevate low-level content to a higher instruction level and grant it greater model-facing privilege. We introduce instruction privilege escalation. In this attack, an attacker induces an agent to elevate low-level malicious content to a higher instruction level. The elevated content then causes the agent to execute instructions it would not follow at their original level. We evaluate this threat by using multi-agent mechanisms to achieve 13 attack objectives across six coding-agent harnesses. These objectives span confidentiality, integrity, availability, and remote code execution. With unrestricted action execution, the attacks achieve all 13 objectives on all six harnesses. Under automatic permission review, the attacks achieve all 13 objectives on all three harnesses that provide this mode. We further reproduce the vulnerability using harness-provided persistent goals and scheduled tasks. These results demonstrate the generality of instruction privilege escalation.

</details>


### [223] [Beyond F1: Evaluating Coverage and Failure Recovery in AI Model Security Scanners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27424)
*Qianlong Lan,Vinothini Pandurangan,Anuj Kaul,Indranil Sanyal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过一个包含170个Pickle和PyTorch工件的合成语料库，评估了ModelScan、ModelAudit和Fickling三种静态扫描器，发现ModelAudit在所有135个带标签的恶意/良性家族中均能做出明确安全判定（100%），而Fickling和ModelScan分别只有81.5%和49.6%。虽然ModelScan在做出判定时精确度、召回率和F1均为100%，但在48个恶意家族中未能完成分析，凸显了判定准确性与判定可用性之间的区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统评估指标只刻画扫描器能产生可用安全判定的情况，忽略了扫描器无法完成分析或给出"不适用"结果的场景。随着机器学习工件中恶意/可执行内容的威胁日益突出，需要更精细的评估框架来区分覆盖范围、分析完成度、明确判定等多个维度。

Method: 构建了包含170个Pickle和PyTorch工件、覆盖145个标本家族的合成基准语料库，其中135个具有二元安全真值标签，10个为故意畸形但无标签。明确区分了非N/A覆盖、分析完成、确定性安全判定、非安全发现和不支持结果五类输出，并分别评估ModelScan、ModelAudit和Fickling三种扫描器。

Result: ModelAudit对全部135个标签家族做出明确安全判定（100%），Fickling为81.5%（110个），ModelScan为49.6%（67个）。ModelScan在做出明确判定时达到100%精度、召回率和F1。Fickling未发现任何超出ModelAudit与ModelScan组合之外的独特真阳家族。在ModelScan未能完成分析的48个恶意家族中，ModelAudit和Fickling均产生了与真值一致的检测。

Conclusion: 评估ML工件安全扫描器时必须将判定准确性与判定可得性分离，同时也应区分增量检测覆盖与工具间冗余。ModelScan虽然在能判定时极其精确，但其在大量恶意家族上无法完成分析，这在实际部署中构成严重局限。

Abstract: Static scanners are increasingly used to identify executable or otherwise unsafe content in machine- learning artifacts, yet conventional evaluation metrics characterize only cases where a scanner yields a usable security judgment. We evaluate ModelScan, ModelAudit, and Fickling using a controlled, artifact-backed benchmark on a synthetic corpus of 170 Pickle and PyTorch focused artifacts across 145 specimen families, 135 of which have binary security ground truth and 10 of which are intentionally malformed without labels. We explicitly distinguish non-N/A coverage, analysis completion, definitive security decisions, non-security findings, and unsupported outcomes. On labeled families, ModelAudit produced definitive security decisions for all 135 families (100%), Fickling for 110 (81.5%), and ModelScan for 67 (49.6%). Conditional on making a definitive judgment, ModelScan achieved 100% precision, recall, and F1. Fickling identified no unique true- positive families beyond those found by the combination of ModelAudit and ModelScan. Furthermore, for the 48 malicious families where ModelScan failed to complete its analysis, both ModelAudit and Fickling generated detections consistent with ground truth. These findings underscore the need to separate judgment accuracy from judgment availability, as well as incremental detection coverage from tool-level redundancy.

</details>


### [224] [RedEvoAgent: Automatic Red-Teaming Agent with Experience-Driven Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27439)
*Junjie Zhang,Hui Liu,Kecheng Chen,Xianbo Mo,Changsheng Chen,Haoliang Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出RedEvoAgent，一个黑盒红队智能体，将跨案例攻击轨迹提炼为简洁可读的攻击技能，通过工具有效性分析和决策工具归因实现技能自适应演化，并使用验证棘轮机制保留有效更新，在多个基准和模型上超越固定攻击与现有智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体部署在真实产品执行环境中，越狱攻击可能触发有害工具调用和持久状态改变，风险远超纯文本生成。现有自动红队方法依赖固定攻击，而基于轨迹检索的智能体攻击者存在检索偏差、工具归因不清晰、上下文开销大和可解释性差等问题。

Method: RedEvoAgent的核心包括：(1) 将跨案例攻击轨迹蒸馏为简洁、人类可读的攻击技能；(2) 通过工具有效性分析(Profiling)和决策工具归因(Deciding-Tool Attribution)驱动技能自适应演化；(3) 验证棘轮机制(Validation Ratchet)仅保留能提升验证性能的技能更新，确保演化方向的可靠性。

Result: 在多个基准、目标模型和目标执行环境上，RedEvoAgent在攻击成功率上优于固定攻击和现有智能体基线，同时提高了工具使用效率，并且能够跨攻击者模型和跨目标执行环境迁移。

Conclusion: 通过将经验蒸馏为可读技能并配合严谨的演化机制，RedEvoAgent为LLM智能体安全评估提供了更高效、更可解释且可迁移的自动红队方案。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly deployed in product-level execution harnesses, where jailbreaks can trigger harmful tool use and persistent state changes, creating greater risks than unsafe text generation alone. Existing automatic red-teaming methods often rely on fixed attacks, while recent agentic attackers coordinate multiple jailbreak tools and show stronger potential through trajectory-based retrieval. However, such retrieval can reuse misleading experiences due to retrieval bias and unclear tool credit, and full trajectories add context overhead while reducing interpretability. We propose RedEvoAgent, a black-box red-teaming agent that distills cross-case attack trajectories into a concise, human-readable attack skill. The attack skill adaptively evolves through tool-effectiveness profiling and Deciding-Tool Attribution for skill updates, and a validation ratchet that retains only updates improving validation performance. Experiments on multiple benchmarks, target models, and target execution harnesses show that RedEvoAgent outperforms fixed and agentic baselines, improves tool efficiency, and transfers across attacker models and target execution harnesses.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [225] [Why not to use the Gaussian kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26974)
*Toni Karvonen,Chris J. Oates*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文论证高斯核（又称平方指数核、径向基函数核）不应作为默认核函数使用，原因是其高度光滑性（解析性）会导致条件方差过小引发过度自信，且伴随数值病态问题，需要nugget等技巧补救。作者更广泛地认为所有解析核都应避免使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯核是高斯过程回归中最常用的核函数之一，但作者认为其在实践中存在严重缺陷，应警惕作为默认选择。论文旨在通过两个理论结果揭示其脆弱性，并推广到更一般的解析核类。

Method: 通过两个理论结果论证：(1) 高斯核导致的条件方差极不切实际地小，若用方差量化预测不确定性几乎必然导致灾难性过度自信；(2) 小方差伴随着数值病态条件问题，实际使用需要nugget项等技巧，这实质上改变了原回归或分类模型。进一步指出问题的根源在于核的解析性，对平稳核而言解析性等价于谱密度指数衰减。

Result: 证明高斯核存在两个核心缺陷：条件方差过小导致的过度自信，以及数值病态问题；并将结论推广至所有解析核，指出其谱密度指数衰减的本质特征。

Conclusion: 高斯核及更广泛的解析核应避免作为默认选择；对于平稳核，应优先考虑谱密度非指数衰减（如代数衰减）的核函数，以保证合理的不确定性量化和数值稳定性。

Abstract: Kernels measure similarity or correlation in tasks such as regression and classification. The Gaussian kernel, other names of which include squared exponential and radial basis function kernel, is one of the most popular in Gaussian process regression. We argue that the Gaussian kernel is best avoided and should never be used as a default. The argument rests on two results demonstrating that the Gaussian kernel is extremely brittle. First, the Gaussian kernel gives rise to a conditional variance that is unrealistically small. If the variance is used to quantify predictive uncertainty, catastrophic overconfidence is almost inevitable. Second, a small variance goes hand in hand with numerical ill-conditioning, so that to use the Gaussian kernel in practice requires tricks such as nugget terms that effectively modify the underlying regression or classification model. These problems are caused by the unnatural smoothness of the Gaussian kernel, a fact we are far from the first to take notice of. The problem is not the Gaussian form itself but the analyticity of the kernel: Our argument is more broadly that analytic kernels are best avoided. For stationary kernels analyticity is essentially equivalent to an exponential decay of the spectral density.

</details>


### [226] [TRACE: Retrospective Streaming Generation of Physical Fields under Sparse Structured Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26219)
*Xinyu Zhang,Lihao Chen,Panqi Chen,Lei Cheng,Ting Zhang,Jianlong Li,Shikai Fang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出TRACE框架，一种面向结构感知下的回顾式流式生成重建框架，用于物理场的连续坐标潜在空间中的时序重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式重建方法大多假设固定的批量条件输入，而真实感知系统通常产生结构化流式数据（如探针扫描局部区域、仪器观测移动视场，通信限制可能使整帧缺失），现有方法无法有效处理这类时序稀疏和空间局部化的感知场景。

Method: TRACE在学习的连续坐标潜在空间中执行近似贝叶斯推断：将稀疏离网测量转换为生成式潜在证据，通过卡尔曼式滤波与状态空间时序先验融合，并利用回顾式平滑对观测不足的历史帧进行精化。

Result: 在活性物质、海洋声速场和超新星模拟上的实验表明，在时序稀疏和空间局部化感知协议下，TRACE在重建质量上匹配或超越了逐帧生成重建器、离线时空方法以及流式数据同化基线。

Conclusion: TRACE为流式结构化感知下的物理场重建提供了统一的生成式框架，结合时序先验与回顾式推断，显著提升了稀疏观测条件下的重建能力。

Abstract: Reconstructing continuous physical fields from sparse measurements is central to scientific monitoring, inverse modeling, and digital-twin construction. Generative reconstruction has recently emerged as a promising paradigm for this task by learning data-driven physical priors that complete plausible full fields from limited observations. However, existing methods largely assume fixed, batch conditioning, whereas real sensing systems often produce structured streams: probes scan local regions, instruments observe moving fields of view, and communication constraints may leave entire frames missing. We propose TRACE, a retrospective streaming generative reconstruction framework for physical fields under structured sensing. TRACE performs approximate Bayesian inference in a learned continuous-coordinate latent space, converting sparse off-grid measurements into generative latent evidence, fusing it with a state-space temporal prior through Kalman-style filtering, and refining under-observed past frames via retrospective smoothing. Experiments on active matter, ocean sound-speed fields, and supernova simulations show that TRACE matches or surpasses frame-wise generative reconstructors, offline spatiotemporal methods, and streaming data-assimilation baselines in reconstruction quality under temporally sparse and spatially localized sensing protocols.

</details>


### [227] [A Unified Descriptive-Complexity Framework for Model Selection under Correlated Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26618)
*Yanhang Zhang,Wei Liu,Yuhong Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出描述复杂度信息准则（DCIC），通过Kraft可容许码长正则化大规模候选模型集合，在亚Weibull噪声下利用逼近误差分离建立选择一致性（无需RIP型条件），并给出模型误设定下仍有效的非渐近oracle风险界；该编码原理还能以很小的类别识别代价将异质类别置于统一复杂度尺度上，实现类别-模型恢复与跨类别风险适应，同时提出复杂度引导搜索路径使计算-统计权衡显式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强预测变量依赖和模型类别不确定的场景下，尤其是在存在指数级众多模型时，模型选择变得极具挑战性。现有方法常依赖RIP类条件或对噪声分布有较强假设，难以在线性/非渐近意义上同时处理大规模候选集合与异质模型类别。

Method: 提出了描述复杂度信息准则（DCIC），利用Kraft可容许码长对大规模候选模型集合进行正则化；通过逼近误差分离（approximation-error separation）实现选择一致性，避免依赖RIP型条件；扩展同一种编码原理以在统一复杂度尺度上处理异质类别（仅需少量类别识别代价）；进一步设计了复杂度引导的搜索路径，显式刻画计算量与统计性能之间的权衡。

Result: 建立了亚Weibull噪声下的模型选择一致性及在模型误设定下仍有效的非渐近oracle风险界；在合适的可识别性条件下实现类别-模型恢复与跨类别风险适应；大惩罚项以高概率得到多项式规模的保留搜索区域，较小惩罚则优化oracle风险基准；数值实验表明在强依赖和模型类别不确定性下能实现稳定的支撑恢复和良好的估计性能。

Conclusion: DCIC为强预测依赖与类别不确定下的高维模型选择提供了一种理论上有保证、计算上可实现的统一框架，通过码长正则化同时解决了模型选择一致性、类别间可比性与计算可行性三大问题。

Abstract: Model selection becomes particularly challenging under strong predictor dependence and model-class uncertainty, especially when there are exponentially many models. We propose a Descriptive-Complexity Information Criterion (DCIC) that regularizes large candidate model collections through Kraft-admissible code lengths. Under sub-Weibull noise, we establish selection consistency through approximation-error separation without relying on RIP-type conditions, together with nonasymptotic oracle risk bounds that remain valid under model misspecification. The same coding principle places heterogeneous classes on a common complexity scale at a small additional class-identification cost. This extension yields class--model recovery under suitable identifiability conditions and risk adaptation across classes. We further develop a complexity-guided search path that makes the computation--statistics trade-off explicit. Large penalties yield polynomial-size retained search regions with high probability, whereas smaller penalties sharpen the oracle risk benchmark. Numerical experiments illustrate stable support recovery and favorable estimation performance under strong dependence and model-class uncertainty.

</details>


### [228] [Incremental Recommendation via Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26804)
*Athanasios Vlontzos,David Gustafsson,Michael O'Riordan,Ciarán M. Gilligan-Lee*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对推荐展示资源有限的问题，将生产推荐模型扩展为因果架构，利用已有的holdback数据（无需新数据采集），通过双阈值定位策略解决处理组与对照组归因窗口不匹配的问题，在Spotify数百万用户的大规模A/B测试中将推荐展示量降低7%，且推荐内容消费无显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐展示是有限资源，向本会自然发现内容的用户推荐不会产生增量价值，反而挤占了其他可能有价值的推荐。因此需要识别并避免这类无增量价值的推荐。

Method: 1) 将现有生产推荐模型扩展为因果架构，利用例行实验基础设施中已收集的holdback数据，无需新增数据采集。2) 针对处理组（短期直接响应窗口）与对照组（多日有机流窗口）归因窗口不一致的问题，提出双阈值定位策略：仅当处理流概率高且有机流概率低时才投放推荐。3) 通过处理组与holdback组的联合训练提升模型校准度。

Result: 在Spotify数百万用户的生产级A/B测试中，该策略将推荐展示量降低7%，同时推荐内容消费无统计显著的下降。联合训练提高了处理头相对于生产基线的校准度。

Conclusion: 因果模型相比仅基于观测数据训练的模型能学习到更具泛化能力的表征，联合使用holdback数据训练可提升校准度，且该方法无需额外数据采集即可在生产规模上实现增量价值优化。

Abstract: Recommendation impressions are a finite resource, hence delivering a recommendation to a user who would discover the content organically yields no incremental value and displaces other recommendations that could. We address this by extending an existing production recommendation model to a causal architecture using holdback data that is already collected as part of routine experimentation infrastructure, requiring no new data collection. A central challenge is that attribution windows differ between treated and holdback observations: treated users are attributed a stream within a short direct-response window, while holdback users are attributed organic streams over a multi-day window. This mismatch makes naive treatment-effect subtraction invalid. We resolve this with a dual-threshold targeting policy that delivers a recommendation only when the probability of a treated stream is high and the probability of organic stream is low. In a production-scale A/B test on millions of Spotify users, this policy reduces recommendation impressions by 7% with no statistically significant reduction in overall recommended content consumption. We further show that joint training with holdback data improves calibration of the treated head relative to the production baseline, and argue this can be taken as evidence that causal models learn more generalisable representations than models trained on observational data alone.

</details>


### [229] [Representation Measurements Under Function-Preserving Reparameterizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27020)
*Abdullah Karasan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了列置换平行分析（column-permutation parallel analysis）不满足函数保持的重参数化不变性：其参考分布和所选成分数会在模型函数与观测协方差谱保持不变的情况下发生改变。通过五种模型、三个检索领域和75种变换的系统实证，作者证明了平行分析得出的成分数与决策可能反映的是隐藏坐标的选择，而非模型本身的内在属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的隐藏坐标并非由输入-输出函数唯一确定，因此从表征导出的测量应具有函数保持的基变换不变性。然而，作者发现列置换平行分析违反了这一原则，其成分数选择可能随隐藏坐标的任意选择而变化，这威胁到该方法的有效性和解释性。

Method: 论文采用理论分析与实证验证相结合的方法：(1)理论层面证明数据内部的参考程序无法同时保持每个坐标边际、保持正交等变性和去除坐标间协方差；(2)实证层面在五种模型、三个检索领域、75种变换下评估列置换平行分析，并设计了中心化对照实验来隔离参考分布驱动的效应，同时与正交不变比较器得分进行对比。

Result: 组件数不一致的中位数为0.79，固定阈值决策不一致的中位数为0.26。中心化对照中，1200个成分数中有1141个发生变化（尽管观测谱不变），而独立平行分析种子不改变任何对应决策。相比之下，正交不变比较器得分保持数值稳定且具有相似的留出判别力。

Conclusion: 平行分析导出的成分数和决策可以反映隐藏坐标的选择，而非模型的一个定义良好的属性。因此，基于平行分析的成分数选择方法缺乏函数保持的重参数化不变性，其结论的可靠性和可解释性值得质疑；而正交不变的比较器得分则提供了更稳健的替代方案。

Abstract: Hidden coordinates are not uniquely determined by a language model's input--output function, so representation-derived measurements should be invariant to function-preserving changes of basis. This study shows that column-permutation parallel analysis violates function-preserving reparameterization invariance because its reference distribution and selected component count can change while the model function and observed covariance spectrum remain fixed. More generally, a data-internal reference procedure cannot simultaneously preserve every coordinate marginal, remain orthogonally equivariant, and remove cross-coordinate covariance. Empirically, across five models, three retrieval domains, and 75 transformations, median component-count disagreement is 0.79 and median fixed-threshold decision disagreement is 0.26. A centering-only control isolates the reference-driven effect, with 1,141 of 1,200 component counts changing despite an unchanged observed spectrum, whereas independent parallel analysis seeds change none of the corresponding decisions. By contrast, orthogonally invariant comparator scores remain numerically stable with similar held-out discrimination. Together, these results show that parallel analysis-derived component counts and decisions can reflect hidden-coordinate choice rather than a well-defined property of the model.

</details>


### [230] [Active Diffusion-Based Inference for Ill-Posed Inverse Problems under Incomplete Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27080)
*Jitao Xu,Nobuo Sato,Yaohang Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于主动学习的扩散模型（Diffusion Model, DM）逆问题求解器。该模型学习参数空间到可观测量空间的映射，通过后验不确定性迭代检测和纠正模型误设定，自动发现并学习正确的参数区域，即使初始训练边界不包含真实参数也能实现鲁棒推断，并给出了贝叶斯框架下的自适应域扩增的理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆问题（从实验观测数据估计未知参数）在许多科学与工程应用中至关重要，但由于非线性、噪声和不适定性，这类问题本质上非常困难。现有的逆问题求解方法在初始训练域不包含真实参数时往往失效或产生不可靠的推断，缺乏对模型错误设定（model misspecification）的自适应处理能力。

Method: 提出一种主动扩散模型（active diffusion-based）逆问题求解器。首先训练一个扩散模型学习参数空间与观测空间之间的映射；随后通过后验不确定性指标迭代地检测模型错误设定区域，并主动地发现和扩展参数搜索范围（adaptive domain augmentation），从而引导模型学习到正确的参数空间区域。整个过程具有严格的贝叶斯理论支撑。

Result: 在两类应用中验证了有效性：(1) 一个具有无穷多解的正向问题（toy inverse problem）；(2) 量子色动力学（QCD）核子结构分析中，将量子关联函数参数化到事件观测量（event observables）的逆问题。

Conclusion: 该方法为逆问题求解提供了基于贝叶斯框架的自适应域扩增策略，使得在不完整先验知识条件下仍能获得稳健的推断结果，弥合了先验不足与精确反演之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Many scientific and engineering applications require estimating unknown parameters from experimentally observable data -- an inverse problem that is inherently challenging due to nonlinearity, noise, and ill-posedness. In this paper, we propose an active diffusion-based inverse problem solver. A DM is trained to learn the mapping between the parameter space and the observable space. By iteratively detecting and correcting model misspecification through posterior uncertainty, the method discovers and learns the correct region of parameter space, even when initial training bounds exclude the true parameters. This provides a principled, Bayesian justification for adaptive domain augmentation and ensures robust inference for inverse problems under incomplete prior knowledge. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our inverse solver for a toy inverse problem with infinite solutions, and for the parameterization of the quantum correlation functions to event observables in a Quantum Chromodynamics analysis of nucleon structure.

</details>


### [231] [Recovering Expert Critic-Sourced Network Adjacency between Musical Artists from Acoustic Distributions: A Construct-Validity Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27291)
*Elena Badillo-Goicoechea,Fengfeng He*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文探讨将"评论家相邻关系"（critically adjacency）作为一种新的音乐推荐信号来源。该信号源于专家评论家在散文中对艺术家的明确配对，无需任何用户数据。作者通过最优传输（Wasserstein）距离在声学层面实验，验证了该关系的实证有效性：整体AUC为0.767，多源共识边缘上达到0.865。结论是评论家话语可分解为可复现的"声学核心"与由社会语境驱动的"社会学残余"，为冷启动推荐提供了新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统音乐推荐依赖用户交互（冷启动场景失效）或内在音乐内容，而评论家相邻关系作为一种第三方信号尚未被外部验证。作者旨在填补这一空白，从声学内容角度检验该关系的构建效度，确认其是否扎根于音乐本體而非仅反映社会学语境。

Method: 将艺术家表示为80维Essentia声学描述符的经验分布，通过边际最优传输（Wasserstein）距离建模艺术家间声学邻近性；在艺术家不相交（cold-start）分割下，用集成模型评估评论家相邻关系能否从声学信号中恢复，并以AUC为指标。进一步按评论共识程度和流派类型进行分层评估。

Result: 整体出样本AUC为0.767（95% CI 0.761-0.775）；恢复率随评论共识单调上升，多来源证实的边缘上达到0.865。分层分析显示，紧密边界、场景化流派（如特定场景风格）的恢复率高于宽泛的行业伞形术语。评论家话语由此可分解为"声学核心"与"社会学残余"两部分。

Conclusion: 评论家相邻关系既具备声学可恢复性，又蕴含社会学信息，是冷启动推荐中兼具原则性与可扩展性的信号来源。该工作为音乐信息检索（MIR）与音乐推荐系统（MRS）提供了社会学视角下的新方法与新理论框架。

Abstract: Music recommendation relies primarily on two signals: user-item interactions, which fail in the cold-start regime, and intrinsic musical content, available for any recording. We argue that a third, largely untapped signal is both richer and more principled: critical adjacency, the pairwise relation established when an expert critic explicitly links two artists in long-form prose. It encodes deliberate judgments about which artists belong together. Prior work established its internal validity, showing it recovers coherent, interpretable communities and can match collaborative filtering in user-satisfaction simulations, with no user data. What has been missing is external validation: whether this critic-sourced relation is grounded in the music itself versus sociological context. We test it against acoustic content, reframing the question as one of construct validity.
  Representing artists as empirical distributions over 80 low-level Essentia acoustic descriptors and modeling pairwise proximity via marginal optimal-transport (Wasserstein) distances, we evaluate how far critical adjacency is sonically recoverable under a cold-start, artist-disjoint split. Our ensemble recovers these edges at out-of-sample AUC of 0.767 (95% CI 0.761-0.775). Recoverability rises monotonically with critical consensus, reaching 0.865 on multi-source attested edges. Stratified evaluations align with sociological models of genre: tightly bounded, scene-based genres show higher recoverability than broad industry umbrella terms.
  Critical discourse is thus a rich source of information for recommendation, decomposing into a reproducible "sonic core" and a "sociological remainder" driven by narrative positioning, subcultural context, and canonical placement. The work offers both a scalable cold-start discovery mechanism and a sociologically grounded approach to MIR and MRS research.

</details>


### [232] [A Finite Sample Analysis for Quantile Temporal Difference Learning in Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27313)
*Zijie Cheng,Xiang Li,Yang Peng,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文为表格型分布强化学习中的同步分位数时间差分学习（QTD）建立了全局有限样本保证，通过分离两种稳定性机制（全局比较论证与局部线性化分析），证明步长α_t=c(t+1)^{-a}（a∈(1/2,1)）下最后迭代的波动量级为Õ(T^{-a/2}/√(1-γ))，且与分位数数量无多项式依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布强化学习中QTD算法的有限样本分析此前缺乏全局保证，现有结果通常局限于局部或渐进分析。作者希望建立从任意初始化出发的全局收敛与样本复杂度刻画，并区分局部随机波动与全局样本复杂度两个不同层面的现象。

Method: 将QTD的稳定性论证拆分为两个机制：(1) 全局比较论证——利用奖励累积分布函数的阶单调性和分布Bellman算子的W_∞压缩性，将任意初始化的迭代值引入局部邻域；(2) 局部线性化——对QTD均场进行线性化，其Jacobian是非奇异M-矩阵，借助相关正半群进行方差敏感的鞅分析。

Result: 对步长α_t=c(t+1)^{-a}（a∈(1/2,1)），最后迭代的主导涨落为Õ(T^{-a/2}/√(1-γ))，且与分位数数量无多项式依赖。确定性瞬态和所需预热时间在最坏情形下可依赖最小Bellman目标密度，其量级为m^{-1}。

Conclusion: 结果清晰区分了局部随机波动（与分位数数量和γ的依赖关系）与全局样本复杂度（受Bellman目标密度制约）两种不同层面的复杂度，为QTD算法的收敛性提供了完整的全局视角。

Abstract: We establish a global finite-sample guarantee for synchronous quantile temporal-difference learning (QTD) in tabular distributional reinforcement learning. The proof separates two stability mechanisms. A global comparison argument, based on the order monotonicity of reward cumulative distribution functions and the $W_\infty$ contraction of the distributional Bellman operator, brings an arbitrarily initialized iterate into a local neighborhood. Inside that neighborhood, we linearize the QTD mean field. Its Jacobian is a nonsingular $M$-matrix, and the associated positive semigroup permits a variance-sensitive martingale analysis. For stepsizes $α_t=c(t+1)^{-a}$ with $a\in(1/2,1)$, the leading last-iterate fluctuation is of order $\widetilde O\bigl(T^{-a/2}/\sqrt{1-γ}\bigr)$ and has no polynomial dependence on the number of quantiles. The deterministic transient and the required burn-in can still depend on the smallest Bellman-target density, which is of order $m^{-1}$ in the worst case. The result therefore distinguishes sharply between the local stochastic fluctuation and the global sample complexity.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [233] [Dimension comparison for Student's statistic under symmetric unimodality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26421)
*Jacopo Lenzi*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究Student统计量的双边Edgeworth展开在r=3处的行为，当自归一化和的尾概率在移动边界rn=3+λ/n处评估时，首个非零差分收敛到显式相曲面Hδ(λ)。通过Khintchine尺度混合表示，构造了一个与n无关的紧支撑C∞对称单峰父分布，其Student尾概率在名义水平趋于2{1-Φ(√3)}（从下方）时对所有足够大的n超过等尺度均匀分布的尾概率。与之对比，分位数比序显示均匀父分布最大化所有偶数阶矩及收敛的偶次幂级数。还推导了固定置信度维数展开及n=6处的精确反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Student统计量的Edgeworth展开中，r=3处首个分布敏感项消失，使得该点成为均匀分布与其它父分布比较的临界节点。作者希望揭示在该特殊点附近，均匀分布是否仍是尾概率意义上的极值分布，以及不同删除方案下维数效应如何影响这一比较。

Method: 采用双边Edgeworth展开在移动边界rn=3+λ/n处的渐近分析；利用Khintchine尺度混合表示构造反例父分布；引入分位数比序进行矩与幂级数的极值性分析；推导固定置信度的维数展开，并通过精确计算验证小维数n处的反转现象。

Result: 建立了统一固定、次线性及固定比例删除的首个非零差分极限——相曲面Hδ(λ)，其零曲线在λ=12/35处以切线穿过。构造了紧支撑C∞对称单峰父分布，使得其Student尾在足够大的n下超过均匀分布；同时证明了均匀分布在偶数矩和收敛偶次幂级数的意义上为极值。发现n=6处存在精确的反转现象。

Conclusion: 均匀分布并非在Student尾比较的所有意义下都是极值分布——在r=3临界点附近可通过构造特定父分布超越之，但在矩与幂级数的分位数比序下，均匀分布保持极值性。n=6的精确反转揭示了小样本维度下的复杂相变行为。

Abstract: Let $q_n(r)$ denote the tail probability at $r$ of the self-normalized sum of $n$ independent centered uniform variables. At $r=3$, the first distribution-sensitive term in the two-sided Edgeworth expansion of Student's statistic vanishes. We evaluate the expansion at the common moving boundary $r_n=3+λ/n$ in dimensions $n$ and $n-k$. Uniformly over deletion ranks retaining a fixed positive fraction of observations, the first nonzero difference converges to an explicit phase surface $H_δ(λ)$; its zero curve unifies fixed, sublinear and fixed-fraction deletions, with tangent crossing $12/35$. Through the Khintchine scale-mixture representation, this comparison yields a single compactly supported $C^\infty$ symmetric unimodal parent, independent of $n$, whose Student tail exceeds the equal-scale uniform tail for all sufficiently large $n$ along nominal levels tending to $2\{1-Φ(\sqrt3)\}$ from below. In contrast, a quantile-ratio order shows that the uniform parent maximizes every even moment and every convergent even power series with nonnegative coefficients. We also derive the fixed-confidence dimension expansion and an exact reversal at $n=6$.

</details>


### [234] [Posterior consistency for subdiffusion inverse problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26536)
*Haoyu Lu,Shaokang Zu,Junxiong Jia*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究半线性时间分数阶次扩散方程中初始状态的贝叶斯反演问题，基于Whittle–Matérn过程的重新缩放高斯先验，证明了带有Lipschitz非线性项时解的H^{2+κ}正则性，并建立了预测误差L²范数和参数Sobolev范数的后验收缩率，同时通过小波填充集和KL散度控制证明了极小极大下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间分数阶次扩散方程在异常扩散现象（如多孔介质、生物组织等）中广泛应用，其初始状态的反演是反问题研究的核心课题。在随机噪声观测下从贝叶斯视角系统刻画后验收缩率和极小极大最优性，是该领域尚未充分解决的问题。

Method: 采用贝叶斯反演框架：将基于Whittle–Matérn过程的重新缩放高斯过程作为初始条件的先验；利用非线性项在H^κ范数下的Lipschitz条件建立解的H^{2+κ}正则性估计；基于后验分布推导预测误差和参数的收缩率；通过构造小波填充集并控制KL散度来证明极小极大下界。

Result: 获得了关于样本量的多项式阶后验收缩率，其指数依赖于先验光滑参数与空间维数；同时证明了与收缩率相匹配的极小极大下界，表明所得贝叶斯估计在渐近意义下具有最优性。

Conclusion: 该工作为半线性时间分数阶次扩散方程初始反演问题提供了完整的贝叶斯后验理论保证，既给出了显式的后验收缩率，又通过极小极大下界验证了方法的渐近最优性，填补了该领域非线性情形下贝叶斯反演收敛性分析的空缺。

Abstract: We study the Bayesian recovery of the initial state in a semilinear time-fractional subdiffusion equation from noisy random space-time point observations. A rescaled Gaussian prior based on a Whittle--Matérn process is assigned to the unknown initial condition. We prove the \(H^{2+κ}\)-regularity of the solution when the nonlinearity satisfies a Lipschitz condition in the \(H^κ\)-norm. We then establish posterior contraction rates for the prediction error in the \(L^2\)-norm and for the parameter in Sobolev norms. The rates are polynomial in the sample size, with exponent depending on the prior smoothness and the spatial dimension. Moreover, we prove a minimax lower bound by constructing a wavelet-packing set and controlling the Kullback--Leibler divergences.

</details>


### [235] [How far can symmetry help? Phase transitions and symmetry selection in sparse functional data analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27055)
*Jocelyn Nembe*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究稀疏函数型数据中，协方差面估计的相位转变如何被定义域的对称性所影响。结果表明，循环对称群将采样阈值从 n^{1/(2β)} 降低到 n^{1/(2β)} q^{-1/2}，且该位移存在饱和效应，最终下界为 n^{1/(4β)}——即使使用完整圆群也无法突破该极限。对称性在经典阈值与常数采样之间起到了"对数尺度上一半路程"的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在稀疏函数型数据分析中，协方差面估计存在显著的相位转变现象：观测点数量 m 超过阈值 m^*_n ≈ n^{1/(2β)} 后，风险从二维非参数速率骤降至参数速率 n^{-1}。作者希望探究定义域的对称性（特别是循环群对称）如何改变这一关键阈值及相应的估计风险。

Method: 采用理论分析框架，从风险下界与上界两个方向逼近：(1) 通过Poisson求和公式与核自相关的正定性分析对称群的影响，推导阈值位移公式 m^*_n ≈ n^{1/(2β)} q^{-1/2}；(2) 构造保持正性的统计子类填充以建立一致下界；(3) 对于近似对称情形，将设计平面划分为三个区域，利用Fourier坐标显式刻画对称谱，并用留出法（hold-out）选择最优对称水平；(4) 数值实验验证所有理论定律。

Result: 阈值位移因子为 q^{-1/2}，因对称性作用于可用配对数量（方差二次项），而参数速率地板不受群平均影响。位移存在饱和：当轨道比带宽更细时，缩减因子为 min(q, c_K/h)，此后速率坍塌为一维非参数速率。因此任何旋转对称（含完整圆群）都无法将阈值降至 n^{1/(4β)} 以下，该下限由上界估计器和下界（差多项式因子）双侧逼近。近似对称情形出现三个阶段，第三阶段采样无法弥补近似误差。重参数化定义域不改变阈值。

Conclusion: 对称性是稀疏函数型数据分析中的一个重要结构性工具：它能将相位转变的阈值在经典值 n^{1/(2β)} 与常数采样之间推进一半（对数尺度），最优利用对称性可将阈值降至 n^{1/(4β)} 的理论下限。上界与下界之间的间隙（多项式因子）仍待闭合，对称性给稀疏协方差估计带来了全新的、量化的收益层次。

Abstract: In sparse functional data analysis, where $n$ curves are each observed at $m$ random points, the covariance surface undergoes a sharp phase transition: if the covariance has smoothness $β$, the risk drops from the two-dimensional nonparametric rate to the parametric rate $n^{-1}$ once $m$ exceeds $m^*_n \asymp n^{1/(2β)}$. We determine what a symmetry of the domain does to that transition. A cyclic group of order $q$ preserving process and design displaces the threshold to $n^{1/(2β)}q^{-1/2}$; the exponent is a square root because symmetry acts on the number of usable pairs, which enters the variance quadratically, while the parametric floor is untouched by group averaging. The displacement saturates: once the orbit is finer than the bandwidth the reduction factor is $\min(q,c_K/h)$, by Poisson summation and positive definiteness of the kernel autocorrelation, and beyond that point the rate collapses to the one-dimensional nonparametric rate. Hence no rotation symmetry, even the full circle group, lowers the threshold below $n^{1/(4β)}$; this floor is attained from above by our estimator and from below, up to a polynomial factor, by our lower bounds. Symmetry thus takes one halfway on a logarithmic scale from the classical threshold to constant sampling. The uniform lower bound rests on a positivity-preserving packing of the stationary sub-class; closing the gap is left open. If the symmetry is only approximate, the risk acquires an approximation term and the design plane splits into three regimes, one unreachable by additional sampling; the symmetry spectrum governing it is explicit in Fourier coordinates, and a hold-out procedure selects the symmetry level with leading constant one below saturation and within an absolute constant beyond. All laws above are confirmed numerically. Reparametrisation of the domain, by contrast, leaves the threshold unchanged.

</details>


### [236] [Connecting Riemannian Geometry and Statistical Inference for Correlation Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27209)
*Argyn Kuketayev*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文建立了2019年提出的商仿射度量（quotient-affine metric）与Jennrich 1970年提出的相关矩阵相等性渐近检验之间的联系。作者发现Jennrich统计量背后的二次型恰好是商仿射度量张量的一半，这一恒等式的来源在于消除正态Fisher信息中的边际标准差与商掉对角缩放执行了相同的投影。此外，作者证明对两个独立高斯样本且总体相关矩阵相同时，按有效样本量缩放的测地距离平方依分布收敛于4χ²_d（d=p(p-1)/2），且当p=2时退化为双样本Fisher z检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全秩相关矩阵上的商仿射度量提供了内在黎曼几何结构，但其测地距离没有闭式解，也缺乏解析的渐近零分布。作者希望建立该现代几何方法与经典统计检验（Jennrich检验）之间的理论联系，从而为商仿射度量的测地距离提供可解析的渐近分布，填补这一空白。

Method: 作者通过数学推导揭示商仿射度量张量与Jennrich统计量二次型之间的精确关系（恰好为2倍），其关键洞察在于：从高斯Fisher信息中消除边际标准差所执行的投影，与商掉对角缩放（quotienting out diagonal rescalings）是同一操作。基于此恒等式，作者推导了平方测地距离的渐近零分布，并利用χ²分布给出闭式结果。

Result: Jennrich统计量直接评估局部商仿射二次型。对于两个独立高斯样本且总体相关矩阵相同的零假设下，有效样本量缩放的平方测地距离依分布收敛于4χ²_d，其中d=p(p-1)/2。当p=2时，该结果还原为经典的双样本Fisher z检验。

Conclusion: 该结果将现代黎曼几何方法（商仿射度量）与经典渐近检验（Jennrich检验）统一起来，为相关矩阵的商仿射测地距离提供了解析的渐近零分布，使这一几何框架在统计推断中具备了可操作性和理论依据。

Abstract: The quotient-affine metric gives an intrinsic Riemannian geometry to full-rank correlation matrices, but its geodesic distance has no closed form and we are not aware of an analytic asymptotic null distribution for it. We connect this geometry, introduced in 2019, with Jennrich's 1970 asymptotic test for equality of correlation matrices. The quadratic form underlying Jennrich's statistic is exactly one half of the quotient-affine metric tensor. The identity arises because eliminating marginal standard deviations from Gaussian Fisher information performs the same projection as quotienting out diagonal rescalings. Jennrich's statistic therefore evaluates the local quotient-affine quadratic form directly. Moreover, for two independent Gaussian samples with a common population correlation matrix, the squared geodesic distance, scaled by effective sample size, converges in distribution to $4χ^2_d$, where $d = p(p-1)/2$. For $p=2$, the result reduces to the two-sample Fisher $z$ test.

</details>


### [237] [Conformal Prediction Through the Lens of Hypothesis Testing: Universality, Impossibility, and Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27310)
*Ryan J. Tibshirani,Rina Foygel Barber,Aaditya Ramdas*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文审视了保形预测与置换检验之间的关系，提出了一种较少被探讨的视角：将保形预测视为对n+1个样本联合分布可交换性（而非仅对Y_{n+1}=y这一假设）的置换检验的逆过程。这一视角更贴合传统假设检验的形式化框架，从而能够直接用经典检验理论（Neyman, Lehmann, Scheffé, Kraft, Le Cam等）复现保形预测文献中的基础普适性与不可行性结果，并利用Neyman-Pearson理论推导出保形预测的最优性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保形预测与置换检验的联系虽广为人知，但已有文献通常从"计算Y_{n+1}=y的置换p值并反转"这一角度来阐述。作者认为这一视角未充分契合传统假设检验的形式化框架，因此希望提供一种替代视角——将保形预测视为对样本可交换性原假设的置换检验进行反转——以建立保形预测与经典检验理论之间更紧密的联系。

Method: 作者将保形预测重新诠释为基于n+1个样本联合分布可交换性这一原假设的置换检验的反转。利用假设检验中对偶性（duality between conformal sets and testing），借助Neyman、Lehmann、Scheffé、Kraft、Le Cam等经典检验理论家的结果来重新推导保形预测中的普适性与不可行性结论；同时运用Neyman-Pearson理论证明最优性：对任意(X,Y)联合分布和任意样本量，在交换分布族中具有有效覆盖的所有预测集方法里，最优方法是一个保形预测器，其得分函数为Y|X的条件密度的倒数。

Result: 论文展示了：(1) 通过将保形预测重构为基于可交换性原假设的置换检验反转，保形预测的基础普适性和不可行性结果可以用经典假设检验理论直接复现；(2) 标准Neyman-Pearson理论可用于推导保形预测的最优性，即得分取逆条件密度Y|X的保形预测器在所有满足有效覆盖的方法中具有最高的效率。

Conclusion: 将保形预测视为对可交换性假设的置换检验的反转，不仅更贴近传统假设检验的形式化，还揭示了保形预测的理论基础可以直接从经典检验理论中导出，包括普适性、不可行性以及基于Neyman-Pearson框架的最优性保证。这一视角统一了两条研究主线，为保形预测提供了更坚实的理论根基。

Abstract: The connections between conformal prediction and permutation tests are already widely-known in the literature. Some authors motivate conformal prediction by saying that it computes a permutation p-value for the hypothesis $H_0 : Y_{n+1} = y$, and then inverts this to form a prediction set for $Y_{n+1}$ (i.e., accepts all values $y$ into the prediction set for which the p-value is large). In this paper, we examine an alternative view, which is less well-known: we again cast conformal prediction via the inversion of a permutation test, but for the null of exchangeability of the joint distribution of the $n+1$ samples. This change in perspective, while simple, adheres more closely to traditional formalization in hypothesis testing, which offers several benefits. First, we use the duality between conformal sets and testing to show that foundational universality and impossibility results in the conformal prediction literature can be reproduced directly using classical hypothesis testing theory (due to Neyman, Lehmann, Scheffé, Kraft, Le Cam, and others). Furthermore, we show that an optimality result for conformal prediction can be derived using standard Neyman-Pearson theory: for any joint distribution of the covariates and response $X,Y$, and any sample size, the optimal method for prediction sets---which delivers the most efficient set among all methods with valid coverage for exchangeable distributions---is a conformal predictor whose score is the inverse conditional density of $Y|X$.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [238] [SLM-Conditioned Hierarchical Relation Routing for Labeled Property Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26132)
*Michal Podstawski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种将小型语言模型（SLM）直接集成到图消息选择中的架构——SLM条件化层级关系路由，用于在标签化属性图中结合结构信息与语义信息进行预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图神经网络将节点和关系的异构文本与类别属性表示为静态特征向量，无法动态判断哪些语义证据应影响特定预测目标的消息传播，缺乏语义感知的消息选择能力。

Method: 采用拓扑GNN提供稳定的结构表示与预测锚点；对每个目标节点，将邻居的结构状态、节点属性编码、关系属性编码和关系类型组合为消息；参数高效的SLM处理结构化图软令牌，生成目标条件化路由查询；该查询先在每种关系类型内选择相关消息，再跨关系级摘要路由信息；最终表示作为拓扑锚点的有界残差更新。

Result: 该方法在邻居级和关系类型级均支持可解释分析，通过语言派生的语义信息增强属性图学习中的预测能力。

Conclusion: 该架构为将语言语义与属性丰富的图学习相结合提供了一种通用机制，在保留结构证据的同时允许上下文语义信息修改预测结果。

Abstract: Labeled property graphs combine relational structure with heterogeneous textual and categorical properties attached to both nodes and relationships. Conventional graph neural networks typically represent these properties as static feature vectors, limiting their ability to determine which semantic evidence should influence message propagation for a particular prediction target. We propose SLM-Conditioned Hierarchical Relation Routing, an architecture that integrates a small language model directly into graph message selection. A topology GNN provides a stable structural representation and prediction anchor. For each target node, incident messages combine the neighbor's structural state, node-property encoding, relationship-property encoding, and relationship type. A parameter-efficient SLM processes structured graph soft tokens and produces a target-conditioned routing query. This query first selects relevant messages within each relationship type and subsequently routes information across relation-level summaries. The resulting representation provides a bounded residual update to the topology anchor, preserving structural evidence while allowing contextual semantic information to modify the prediction. The architecture supports interpretable analysis at both the neighbor and relationship-type levels and provides a general mechanism for integrating language-derived semantics into property-rich graph learning.

</details>


### [239] [NeuronFuzz: Safety Neuron Guided Fuzzing for LLM Safety Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26222)
*Zhiyuan Xu,Muhammad Firhard Roslan,Joseph Gardiner,Sana Belguith,Lichao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuronFuzz是一个白盒模糊测试框架，利用大语言模型内部的安全神经元激活作为连续执行反馈，通过构建可微分的SafetyOracle在prefill阶段获取安全报警分数，消除了响应生成环节，从而高效地发现越狱攻击模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动化越狱测试方法依赖响应级反馈，每个候选提示都需要生成目标模型响应来评估攻击效果，这不仅成本高昂，而且在强对齐模型上由于大多数候选都被拒绝（失败结果相同），只能提供稀疏的指导信号，难以有效引导搜索。

Method: NeuronFuzz包含三个关键组件：1) SafetyOracle：利用模板不变的有害/良性输入和稳定性感知选择，识别一组紧凑的安全神经元，将其激活转换为连续的安全报警分数作为模糊测试反馈，并可在prefill阶段获得；2) 基于梯度的方法识别安全敏感模板位置；3) 使用掩码语言模型生成流畅且上下文兼容的变异，同时保留原始有害载荷并避免额外的优化变量。

Result: 在21个文本和多模态模型上评估，五个白盒源模型上达到76-100%的越狱发现率，比基线高出最多48个百分点；优化后的模板零样本迁移到开放权重和六个专有目标模型，平均ASR和top-5集成ASR分别达到69.6%/92.6%和44.1%/60.0%。

Conclusion: NeuronFuzz证明通过利用安全神经元作为连续反馈信号，可以在无需生成完整响应的前提下高效地发现越狱攻击模板，且具有良好的跨模型迁移能力，为LLM安全评估提供了一种更高效且可扩展的方法。

Abstract: Safety evaluation is critical for assessing whether aligned Large Language Models (LLMs) remain robust against jailbreak attacks. Existing automated testing methods, however, largely rely on response-level feedback: each candidate prompt typically requires generating a target-model response to evaluate its attack effectiveness. This process is expensive and, more importantly, provides only sparse guidance on strongly aligned models, where most candidates are rejected with the same failure outcome.
  This paper presents NeuronFuzz, a white-box fuzzing framework that exploits internal safety neurons as continuous execution feedback for LLM safety evaluation. A SafetyOracle converts safety-neuron activations into a continuous safety alarm score that serves as feedback for fuzzing and can be obtained during prefill, eliminating response generation from the fuzzing loop. To construct the SafetyOracle, NeuronFuzz uses template-invariant harmful and benign inputs and stability-aware selection to identify a compact set of safety neurons whose activations capture harmful-intent recognition. Moreover, since the safety alarm score is differentiable, NeuronFuzz uses its gradients to identify safety-sensitive template positions and a masked language model to generate fluent, context-compatible mutations while preserving original harmful payload and avoiding additional optimization variables. We evaluate NeuronFuzz across 21 text and multimodal models. Across five white-box source models, it achieves a 76-100% jailbreak discovery rate, outperforming baselines by up to 48 percentage points. Its optimized templates further transfer zero-shot to open-weight and six proprietary target models, achieving average ASR and top-5 ensemble ASR (EASR) of 69.6%/92.6% and 44.1%/60.0%, respectively.

</details>


### [240] [Pruning Binarized Neural Networks: A Dedicated Framework and Globally Weighted Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26233)
*Roan Rubiales,Jean Pierre David*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于PyTorch的研究型框架，用于二值化神经网络（BNN）的设计与优化，集成了冻结和剪枝机制。基于该框架，作者提出了一种跨抽象层次考虑参数相对重要性的全局加权剪枝方法，在VGG11上实现了70%的剪枝率且精度不变，远超二值化设置下现有最优的41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型的极端压缩（如全二值化）能大幅降低内存占用和算术复杂度，便于在FPGA和微控制器等边缘硬件上部署。虽然将二值化与剪枝结合有望带来额外的效率提升，但现有的剪枝策略不适用于二值化表示，且很少能转化为实际的硬件资源节省。

Method: 开发了一个基于PyTorch的研究导向框架，集成了冻结（freezing）与剪枝（pruning）机制，用于设计和优化二值化神经网络。在此框架上提出了一种新的剪枝方法，该方法在不同抽象层次上考虑学习参数的相对重要性，实现全局加权剪枝机制。

Result: 该全局加权机制在模型精度与剪枝率之间取得了优越的平衡：在VGG11上实现了70%的剪枝率且精度保持恒定，而二值化设置下的现有最优结果仅达到41%。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架和全局加权剪枝方法能够显著提升二值化神经网络的压缩效率，超越了现有技术在该领域的表现，为边缘设备上高效部署深度神经网络提供了更好的解决方案。

Abstract: Extreme compression of deep neural networks, up to full binarization, dramatically reduces memory footprint and arithmetic complexity, facilitating deployment on constrained edge hardware with field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs) and microcontrollers. Although combining binarization with pruning promises additional efficiency gains, existing pruning strategies are ill-suited to binarized representations and rarely translate into meaningful hardware savings. We introduce a PyTorch-based, research-oriented framework that incorporates freezing and pruning mechanisms for designing and optimizing binarized neural networks. The framework enables rapid and reproducible evaluation of state-of-the-art approaches and the fast prototyping of new ones. Leveraging this framework, we propose a novel pruning method that accounts for the relative importance of learned parameters across abstraction levels. Such a global weighting mechanism consistently achieves a superior trade-off between model accuracy and pruning rate, achieving a 70% pruning rate on VGG11 with constant accuracy, while state-of-the-art results reach only 41% in the binarized setting.

</details>


### [241] [Muon with Finite Newton-Schulz: The Smoothing Benefit in Nonsmooth Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26288)
*Mingyi Li,Taira Tsuchiya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明有限深度Newton-Schulz迭代不仅不是Muon优化器的近似误差来源，反而是其收敛性保证的关键。通过online-to-nonconvex转化框架，指出有限Newton-Schulz将不连续极坐标映射平滑化为Lipschitz映射，使Muon可视为具有平滑谱势的在线学习器，从而在非光滑非凸优化中获得收敛保证，而使用精确极坐标更新的Muon可能不收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon是大语言模型预训练中针对矩阵值参数的高效优化器，其核心是使用几次Newton-Schulz迭代近似正交化动量。然而现有理论要么用精确极坐标分解替代该迭代，要么将有限迭代深度视为近似误差，导致Muon实际运行的有限深度迭代只会损害理论保证。这与实践中Muon表现优异的事实相矛盾，亟需一个能解释有限Newton-Schulz迭代为何有益的严谨理论。

Method: 本文通过online-to-nonconvex转化框架分析Muon：将更新规则视为在线学习器，将其遗憾界转化为驻点保证。核心洞察是有限Newton-Schulz迭代将不连续的极坐标映射平滑化为关于奇异值的Lipschitz映射，从而使Muon成为具有平滑谱势的在线学习者。作者证明Newton-Schulz深度只需随目标精度对数增长即可保证非光滑非凸优化的收敛性，并将论证推广到具有相同平滑性质的一般谱映射。

Result: 证明了有限Newton-Schulz迭代对收敛有益而非有害：深度仅需在目标精度上呈对数增长即可收敛到非光滑非凸优化的驻点；而精确极坐标更新的Muon可能不收敛。样本复杂度界与非光滑非凸优化的已知最优界匹配，且在光滑非凸优化中达到与问题相关因子无关的最优性。论证可扩展到满足平滑性质的一般谱映射。

Conclusion: 该工作颠覆了以往将有限Newton-Schulz视为近似误差的观点，揭示了其平滑化作用对非光滑非凸优化的收敛性至关重要，从理论层面解释了Muon在实践中广泛成功的原因，并为设计基于谱映射的优化器提供了新的理论指导。

Abstract: Muon has emerged as a strong optimizer for the matrix-valued parameters in large language model pretraining, approximately orthogonalizing its momentum with a few Newton-Schulz iterations. Existing theory either replaces this iteration with the exact polar factor it approximates, or treats its finite depth as an approximation error, and thus the iteration Muon actually runs can only hurt the guarantees. We show that finite Newton-Schulz can instead be beneficial for nonsmooth nonconvex optimization. To this end, we analyze Muon through the online-to-nonconvex conversion, which views the update rule as an online learner and converts its regret bound into a stationarity guarantee. The finite Newton-Schulz iteration smooths the discontinuous polar map into a Lipschitz map of the singular values, and Muon with finite Newton-Schulz can be regarded as an online learner with a smoothed spectral potential. This smoothing is exactly what the conversion needs: we prove that a Newton-Schulz depth growing only logarithmically in the target accuracy suffices for convergence to stationary points in nonsmooth nonconvex optimization, whereas Muon with the exact-polar update may fail to converge. The resulting sample complexity bounds match the best-known guarantees for nonsmooth nonconvex optimization and are optimal for smooth nonconvex optimization up to problem-dependent factors. The argument extends beyond Newton-Schulz to general spectral maps with the same smoothing property.

</details>


### [242] [Algebraic Multigrid Acceleration for Efficient Label Spreading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26309)
*Antonia van Betteray,Jonathan Klees,Miriam Schäfers,Matthias Rottmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AMELS框架，利用代数多重网格求解器加速标签传播（label spreading）在半监督学习中的应用，显著降低大规模高维数据集上的计算和内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模数据集的标注成本高昂，标签传播作为半监督学习技术虽有效，但在大规模高维数据集上受限于计算成本和内存约束，难以扩展。

Method: AMELS通过两方面改进标签传播的可扩展性：(1) 快速构建近邻图；(2) 引入代数多重网格（AMG）迭代求解器，替代传统的随机游走迭代。利用AMG的多层特性，在单个多重网格循环中即可在任意规模的图上传播标签信息。

Result: AMELS相比现有实现在运行时间上实现了显著降低，同时对超参数选择更具鲁棒性（在运行时间和分类准确率两方面）。在仅有少量标注样本时仍能在大规模图像数据集上产生准确标签。

Conclusion: AMELS使得标签传播能够高效应用于大规模图像数据集，在标注样本稀缺的情况下仍能获得准确的标签结果，为半监督学习在大规模场景下的部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Modern machine learning models rely on large amounts of labeled data. However, manual annotation of large-scale datasets is expensive and time-consuming. Label spreading is a semi-supervised learning technique that addresses this challenge by propagating information from a few labeled examples to a larger pool of unlabeled data. Despite its effectiveness, its application to large-scale, high-dimensional datasets is limited by computational costs and memory constraints. To address these limitations, we propose Algebraic Multigrid Acceleration for Efficient Label Spreading (AMELS), an efficient label spreading framework that improves scalability by fast construction of neighborhood graphs and the incorporation of algebraic multigrid solvers. The latter is an iterative solver that replaces the ordinary random walk iteration typically performed in label spreading. Due to the multilevel nature of algebraic multigrid solvers, AMELS spreads given label information across a graph of any size in a single multigrid cycle. We demonstrate that AMELS achieves significant runtime reductions compared to existing implementations while also being more robust to hyperparameter choices in terms of both runtime and classification accuracy. Our framework therefore enables efficient label spreading on large-scale image datasets and produces accurate labels even when only a few labeled samples are available.

</details>


### [243] [Privacy Without Regret: Differentially Private Inference-Time Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26324)
*Ishi Jain,Nandini Bhattad,Sayak Ray Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种隐私保护的推理时对齐方法：PrivBoN通过在奖励分数上添加Gumbel噪声同时实现差分隐私和KL正则化对齐，而PrivITP通过χ²正则化拒绝采样与两阶段高斯机制实现更强的隐私保证，两者在实验中均优于传统Best-of-N采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Best-of-N采样是最广泛使用的推理时对齐策略，但存在两个问题：一是奖励黑客（reward hacking），即选中的响应利用了代理奖励模型的错误；二是训练奖励模型所用的人类偏好数据缺乏隐私保护。本文旨在通过单一干预（添加校准噪声）同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 本文提出两种方法：(1) Private Best-of-N (PrivBoN)：在奖励分数选择前添加适当尺度的Gumbel噪声，该噪声同时提供ε-差分隐私并实现KL正则化对齐；(2) Private Inference-Time Pessimism (PrivITP)：结合χ²正则化拒绝采样与两阶段高斯机制，实现ex-post (ε,δ)-DP，隐私成本与响应数量n无关，并将正则化参数与隐私参数解耦。

Result: 理论结果表明，当隐私预算超过临界阈值ε*时，隐私强制噪声即为遗憾最优的正则化，隐私引入零额外对齐成本，匹配Huang等人(2025)的信息论下界。实验显示PrivBoN和PrivITP具有缩放单调性（而BoN在超过临界n后性能退化），且PrivITP在同等隐私水平下匹配或优于PrivBoN，在强隐私场景下收益最大。

Conclusion: 添加校准噪声这一单一干预即可同时解决BoN的奖励黑客和隐私泄露两大问题，实现隐私保护与对齐性能的帕累托最优。PrivITP在强隐私场景下表现最优，为推理时对齐提供了可扩展且隐私安全的方案。

Abstract: Best-of-N (BoN) sampling is the simplest and most widely deployed inference-time alignment strategy, but it suffers from two distinct problems: reward hacking, in which the selected response exploits errors in the proxy reward model, and the absence of any privacy protection for the sensitive human preference data used to train that reward model. We show that a single intervention-adding calibrated noise to reward scores before selection-resolves both. Our first result, Private Best-of-N (PrivBoN), establishes that Gumbel noise at an appropriate scale simultaneously provides $ε$-differential privacy and implements KL-regularized alignment. Whenever the privacy budget exceeds a critical threshold $ε^*$, the privacy-mandated noise is the regret-optimal regularization, and privacy imposes zero additional alignment cost-matching the information-theoretic skyline of Huang et al. (2025). Because $ε^*$ depends on an unknown coverage coefficient, we introduce Private Inference-Time Pessimism (PrivITP), which combines $χ^2$-regularized rejection sampling with a two-phase Gaussian mechanism. PrivITP achieves ex-post $(ε,δ)$-DP with a privacy cost independent of the number of responses $n$, cleanly decouples the regularization parameter from the privacy parameter, and attains the skyline up to a noise-inflation term. Experiments across several language models, datasets, and reward models confirm our results: PrivBoN and PrivITP are scaling-monotonic (unlike BoN, which degrades past a critical $n$), and PrivITP matches or outperforms PrivBoN at equivalent privacy levels, with the largest gains in the strong-privacy regime.

</details>


### [244] [Beyond Capability Benchmarks: Learning Operational Fingerprints of LLM Cloud Services from Production Incident Metadata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26332)
*Meiwei Zhang,Eduardo Miranda,Bruce Baynes,Suvigya Jain,Wanlong Chen,Tao He,Sergey Borodavkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 OpEmbed 框架，从隐私保护的工单元数据中学习 LLM 云服务的高层运维指纹（operational fingerprint），无需使用工单文本。该框架在 Google Cloud 26个月内 7 个 LLM 家族、33,000+ 生产支持案例上验证，可恢复可解释的家族和版本级结构，改进 leave-one-model-out 运维预测，并在有限早期数据和跨模型故障类型迁移下保持有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 托管 LLM 服务已进入生产系统，但模型选择和服务规划仍依赖能力基准（capability benchmarks），这些基准对部署后的运行行为（operational behavior）揭示甚少。现有方法缺乏基于运维数据的、可用于模型选择和服务的量化框架，且需保护隐私、避免依赖文本级支持案例内容。

Method: OpEmbed 将模型-时间窗口聚合为八通道运维签名（operational signature），通过时间对比学习、跨视图重构和代际序正则化（generational-ordinality regularization）学习低维表示。该方法仅使用结构化的隐私保护工单元数据，不使用案例文本。

Result: 在 Google Cloud 26个月、7个LLM家族、33,000多生产支持案例上，OpEmbed 可恢复可解释的家族和版本级结构，优于非学习的基线方法在 leave-one-model-out 运维预测中的表现，在有限的早期窗口数据下依然有效，并支持跨模型故障类型迁移。

Conclusion: OpEmbed 为模型接入（onboarding）、支持就绪评估和运维监控提供了实用的工程工具与经验教训，表明从结构化工单元数据学习运维指纹是可行且有效的。

Abstract: Managed LLM services are now part of real production systems, but model selection and service planning still rely heavily on capability benchmarks that reveal little about operational behavior after deployment. We present Operational Embedding (OpEmbed), a framework for learning compact operational fingerprints of LLM cloud services from structured, privacy-preserving support-case metadata, without using case text. OpEmbed aggregates model--time windows into an eight-channel operational signature and learns a low-dimensional representation via temporal contrastive learning, cross-view reconstruction, and generational-ordinality regularization. Evaluated on more than 33,000 production support cases spanning seven LLM families over 26 months at Google Cloud, OpEmbed recovers interpretable family- and version-level structure, improves leave-one-model-out operational forecasting over non-learned baselines, remains useful under limited early-window data, and supports cross-model fault-type transfer. We report the practical lessons learned from building and evaluating this tool for model onboarding, support readiness assessment, and operational monitoring.

</details>


### [245] [CG4AI: A Column Generation Framework for Training AI Models Under Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26375)
*Youcef Magnouche,Abderrahmane Driouch,Sébastien Martin,Pierre Bauguion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CG4AI框架，通过构建AI模型的凸组合并在组合输出上强制执行线性约束，利用主线性规划确定最优混合权重、定价子问题生成新模型、切割平面过程扩展可行性保证。在MNIST数字分类和多商品流问题上验证，该方法能产生可行预测器且比单模型基线精度更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的机器学习训练仅最小化数据集上的损失函数，无法保证模型输出满足预定义的规则或约束。但在许多现实应用中（如自主系统和网络路由），这种可行性保证至关重要。

Method: CG4AI框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 主线性规划(LP)确定最优模型混合权重；(2) 定价子问题利用LP对偶变量生成新模型，聚焦于最被违反的约束；(3) 切割平面过程将可行性保证扩展到训练集之外。该方法本质上借鉴了列生成(column generation)的思想应用于AI模型组合。

Result: 在MNIST上展示了约束的四种用途：仅从约束学习、提高对抗鲁棒性、修正错误分类样本、强制执行输出重标记。在多商品流问题中，在神经网络路由预测器上强制链路容量约束。实验表明CG4AI可靠地产生可行预测器，且在精度上优于单模型基线。

Conclusion: CG4AI通过凸组合多个AI模型并施加线性约束，成功地在保证输出可行性的同时提升了预测精度，验证了该方法在分类和网络路由等不同领域问题上的广泛适用性。

Abstract: Standard machine-learning training minimizes a loss function over a dataset, but does not guarantee that the resulting model will satisfy predefined rules or constraints on its outputs. In many real-world applications, ranging from autonomous systems to network routing, such guarantees are essential. We propose CG4AI, a framework that builds a convex combination of AI models while enforcing linear constraints on the combined output. A master linear program (LP) determines the optimal mixture weights, while a pricing subproblem generates new models guided by LP dual variables, focusing attention on the most violated constraints. A cutting-plane procedure extends feasibility guarantees beyond the training set. We apply CG4AI to two problems: (i) digit classification on MNIST, where we demonstrate four distinct uses of constraints, learning from constraints alone, improving adversarial robustness, correcting misclassified examples, and enforcing output relabeling; and (ii) the multi-commodity flow problem, where link capacity constraints are enforced on neural-network routing predictors. Experiments on MNIST and standard SNDLIB benchmark networks show that CG4AI reliably produces feasible predictors while achieving better accuracy than single-model baselines.

</details>


### [246] [The Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy: A Framework for Constructing Classifiers and Diagnosing Their Decisions, Applied to Prompt Injection Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26423)
*Jaturong Kongmanee,Smile Thanapattheerakul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个名为"潜在诊断分类法"(Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy)的安全防护框架，构建一个可诊断的分类器防护层，用于识别分类器自信决策中哪些可以被信任。框架包含维度优化分类器构建、潜在支持向量定位、诊断分类法构建三个步骤，将提示分为安全依赖、启发式偏差/覆盖、以及需要人工审查的上下文不足三类。在公共提示注入数据集上，发现约77%的自信决策在移除单个词元后不稳定，并区分为置信度校准失败和可利用捷径两种失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类器在安全防护中的决策并不总是可靠的，尤其是当其对输入做出"自信"的判断时，这种自信可能源于虚假的信号（如启发式捷径）而非真实语义。需要一个系统性的框架来区分哪些自信决策值得信赖，哪些需要进一步处理。

Method: 框架包含三个步骤：(i) 通过交叉验证选择最佳嵌入维度构建维度优化分类器（而非预先固定维度）；(ii) 定位约29%训练样本作为潜在支持向量，识别能改变分类器预测标签的关键词元；(iii) 利用这些词元及其攻击幅度构建诊断分类法，将提示分为Safely Rely、Heuristic Bias/Override、Insufficient Context三个区域，并为每个区域推荐修复策略。

Result: 在公共提示注入数据集上，约77%的自信决策对移除单个词元不鲁棒。这种脆弱性可区分为两种不同失败模式：置信度校准失败（confidence calibration failure）和真正可被利用的捷径（exploitable shortcut）。框架为每个分类法区域给出了对应处理策略。

Conclusion: 该工作表明分类器的自信决策可能高度脆弱，通过潜在诊断分类法可以系统性地识别并分类这些脆弱性，从而为不同的提示分配不同的处理路径（直接信任、标记启发式偏差/覆盖、或转交人工审查），为安全防护层的构建提供了可操作的分步指南。

Abstract: This paper proposes a framework for constructing a classifier as a safeguard layer, and for developing a complementary diagnostic that identifies which of the classifier's confident decisions can be trusted. This framework, the Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy, consists of (i) constructing a dimensionality-optimized classifier, in which the embedding dimensionality is empirically selected via cross-validated performance rather than fixed a priori, (ii) locating a relatively small set of latent support vectors (~ 29% of total training examples) representing influential prompts for identifying tokens that alter the classifier's predicted labels, and (iii) utilizing such tokens and their associated attack magnitudes for constructing a diagnostic taxonomy. This diagnostic taxonomy provides an end-to-end guideline for flagging prompts that require different treatments: rely Safely on the classifier's decision; flag Heuristic Bias and Heuristic Override cases; route Insufficient Context cases for further human/safety review. Applying the framework to a classifier trained on a public prompt injection dataset, we find that a substantial fraction of its confident decisions (~ 77%) are not robust to removing a single token, and that this brittleness separates into two distinct failure patterns: a confidence calibration failure and a genuinely exploitable shortcut. For each zone of the taxonomy, we also recommend strategies for remediating diagnosed prompts. We illustrate the framework as a series of steps, demonstrating how each step operates.

</details>


### [247] [FedCMAPSS: A Benchmark for Federated Learning in Remaining Useful Life Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26433)
*Amelia Sorrenti,Matteo Pennisi,Concetto Spampinato,Simone Palazzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedCMAPSS，一个基于NASA C-MAPSS数据集的联邦剩余使用寿命（RUL）估计基准，定义了五个标准化任务以模拟真实工业场景（从IID到极端统计异质性），并对多种联邦优化算法和神经架构进行了系统评估，公开了源代码和数据划分以建立可复现的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习为在不共享传感器数据的情况下协作训练RUL预测模型提供了有前景的范式，但该领域缺乏统一的评估框架，导致不同研究结果难以公平比较。

Method: 基于NASA C-MAPSS数据集构建FedCMAPSS基准，设计五个标准化任务涵盖从理想IID到极端统计异质性的数据分布场景，并在多种神经架构上系统评估最先进的联邦优化算法。

Result: 建立了可复现的联邦RUL估计基线，提供了标准化任务和公开可用的源代码与数据划分，使得不同联邦学习方法可以进行公平比较。

Conclusion: FedCMAPSS为联邦预测性维护研究提供了标准化的评估基础，有助于推动该领域的发展，使联邦RUL估计模型的开发和比较更加系统和可复现。

Abstract: Data-driven prognostics and health management has emerged as a key enabler for Industry 4.0, yet the development of robust remaining useful life (RUL) estimation models is often limited by the scarcity of run-to-failure data. While federated learning offers a promising paradigm to collaboratively train predictive models without sharing sensor data, research efforts have operated so far in the absence of a common evaluation framework. To address this gap, this paper introduces FedCMAPSS, a benchmark for federated RUL estimation based on the commonly-used NASA C-MAPSS dataset. We define a set of five standardized tasks designed to simulate real-world industrial challenges, ranging from ideal IID settings to extreme statistical heterogeneity, and conduct a systematic evaluation of state-of-the-art federated optimization algorithms across multiple neural architectures. By establishing reproducible baselines and making the source code and data splits publicly available, this work aims to provide a standard foundation for developing and comparing federated predictive maintenance solutions.

</details>


### [248] [NeoTriFuse: Reliability-Aware Multimodal Fusion under Missingness Heterogeneity for Neonatal Mortality Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26436)
*Jiyuan Tian,Qincheng Shen,Ye Lin,Yu Gao,Haohui Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NeoTriFuse，一个可靠性感知的多模态融合框架，用于处理新生儿死亡率预测中的数据缺失问题。该框架将缺失性建模为明确的可靠性信号，而非仅仅作为预处理问题，通过可靠性引导的门控机制动态调节模态贡献，联合优化死亡率预测与住院时长辅助目标，在现实临床缺失条件下取得了具有竞争力的性能（F1=0.6736，AUROC=0.9454），消融实验表明局部-全局时序架构和患者级统计摘要分支贡献最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新生儿死亡率预测面临极端类别不平衡、临床风险因素异质性、多尺度时间动态和大量数据缺失等挑战。传统多模态方法将缺失性仅视为预处理问题，未能充分利用缺失模式所蕴含的可靠性信息，因此需要一种能够将缺失性本身作为显式可靠性信号来动态调节多模态融合的新方法。

Method: NeoTriFuse框架包含三个主要组成部分：(1)静态围产期变量输入；(2)局部-全局时间编码器用于捕获多尺度时间动态；(3)患者级统计摘要。通过可靠性引导的门控机制动态调节各模态在融合中的贡献，并将死亡率预测与辅助的住院时长预测目标进行联合优化。

Result: NeoTriFuse在新生儿死亡率预测任务上取得F1分数0.6736±0.0216和AUROC 0.9454±0.0056。消融实验显示局部-全局时序架构和患者级统计摘要分支对预测性能贡献最大，可靠性感知门控在异构观测完整性条件下对阈值依赖指标有额外提升，敏感性分析表明在邻近超参数设置下性能稳定。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在现实临床缺失条件下，可靠性感知的多模态融合方法是新生儿死亡率预测的实用方案，能够有效利用缺失模式中的信息来提升预测性能。

Abstract: Neonatal mortality risk prediction from bedside monitoring data remains challenging due to extreme class imbalance, heterogeneous clinical risk factors, multi-scale temporal dynamics, and substantial missingness. We propose NeoTriFuse, a reliability-aware multimodal fusion framework for missingness-heterogeneous neonatal monitoring data.
